```mql5
        //=================================================================
        
        KernelBuffer[i] = CalculateKernelRegression(i, close);
        
        //=================================================================
        // FRACTAL DETECTION
        //=================================================================
        
        if(i >= 5) // Need at least 5 bars
        {
            bool isFractalHigh = DetectFractalHigh(i, high, InpFractalFilter);
            bool isFractalLow = DetectFractalLow(i, low, InpFractalFilter);
            
            if(isFractalHigh)
            {
                int shift = (InpFractalFilter == "3") ? 1 : 2;
                AddFractalHigh(high[i + shift], time[i + shift]);
            }
            
            if(isFractalLow)
            {
                int shift = (InpFractalFilter == "3") ? 1 : 2;
                AddFractalLow(low[i + shift], time[i + shift]);
            }
        }
    }
    
    //--- Update ZigZag on last bar
    if(prev_calculated > 0)
    {
        UpdateZigZag(time, high, low, close, rates_total);
    }
    
    //--- Update Orderblocks
    if(prev_calculated > 0)
    {
        UpdateOrderBlocks(time, open, high, low, close, rates_total);
    }
    
    //--- Draw objects on chart
    if(rates_total > prev_calculated)
    {
        DrawSupportResistance(high, low, close, rates_total);
        DrawTradeSetup(close[0], time[0]);
        DrawTable();
    }
    
    return(rates_total);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate True Range                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateTrueRange(int shift, const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                          const double &close[], bool modified, int period)
{
    if(shift >= ArraySize(high) - 1)
        return 0;
    
    if(modified)
    {
        // Modified True Range calculation
        double sma_hl = 0;
        int count = 0;
        for(int i = shift; i < shift + period && i < ArraySize(high); i++)
        {
            sma_hl += (high[i] - low[i]);
            count++;
        }
        if(count > 0)
            sma_hl /= count;
        
        double HiLo = MathMin(high[shift] - low[shift], 1.5 * sma_hl);
        
        double HRef = (low[shift] <= high[shift+1]) ? 
                      (high[shift] - close[shift+1]) : 
                      (high[shift] - close[shift+1] - 0.5 * (low[shift] - high[shift+1]));
        
        double LRef = (high[shift] >= low[shift+1]) ? 
                      (close[shift+1] - low[shift]) : 
                      (close[shift+1] - low[shift] - 0.5 * (low[shift+1] - high[shift]));
        
        return MathMax(HiLo, MathMax(HRef, LRef));
    }
    else
    {
        // Standard True Range
        double tr1 = high[shift] - low[shift];
        double tr2 = MathAbs(high[shift] - close[shift+1]);
        double tr3 = MathAbs(low[shift] - close[shift+1]);
        
        return MathMax(tr1, MathMax(tr2, tr3));
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Wilder's Moving Average                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double WildMA(double value, int period, int shift)
{
    static double prev = 0;
    
    if(shift == 0 || prev == 0)
    {
        prev = value;
        return value;
    }
    
    prev = prev + (value - prev) / period;
    return prev;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 1                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine1(int shift, const double &close[], const double &high[], 
                          const double &low[], const double &atr[])
{
    static double TrendLine1_prev = 0;
    static int iTrend1_prev = 0;
    
    // Calculate BB Upper and Lower
    double sma = 0;
    double sum = 0;
    int count = 0;
    
    for(int i = shift; i < shift + InpBBPeriod1 && i < ArraySize(close); i++)
    {
        sma += close[i];
        count++;
    }
    if(count > 0)
        sma /= count;
    
    // Calculate standard deviation
    for(int i = shift; i < shift + InpBBPeriod1 && i < ArraySize(close); i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[i] - sma, 2);
    }
    double stdev = (count > 0) ? MathSqrt(sum / count) : 0;
    
    double BBUpper1 = sma + stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    double BBLower1 = sma - stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    
    // Determine BB Signal
    int BBSignal1 = 0;
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper1)
        BBSignal1 = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower1)
        BBSignal1 = -1;
    
    // Calculate TrendLine1
    double TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    
    if(BBSignal1 == 1)
    {
        if(InpUseATRFilter1)
            TrendLine1 = low[shift] - atr[shift];
        else
            TrendLine1 = low[shift];
        
        if(TrendLine1 < TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal1 == -1)
    {
        if(InpUseATRFilter1)
            TrendLine1 = high[shift] + atr[shift];
        else
            TrendLine1 = high[shift];
        
        if(TrendLine1 > TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    
    // Determine trend
    int iTrend1 = iTrend1_prev;
    if(TrendLine1 > TrendLine1_prev)
        iTrend1 = 1;
    else if(TrendLine1 < TrendLine1_prev)
        iTrend1 = -1;
    
    // Detect signals
    if(iTrend1_prev == -1 && iTrend1 == 1 && !InpHideLabels1)
        BuySignal1Buffer[shift] = TrendLine1 - atr[shift];
    else
        BuySignal1Buffer[shift] = EMPTY_VALUE;
    
    if(iTrend1_prev == 1 && iTrend1 == -1 && !InpHideLabels1)
        SellSignal1Buffer[shift] = TrendLine1 + atr[shift];
    else
        SellSignal1Buffer[shift] = EMPTY_VALUE;
    
    // Update buffer
    TrendLine1Buffer[shift] = TrendLine1;
    
    // Save for next iteration
    TrendLine1_prev = TrendLine1;
    iTrend1_prev = iTrend1;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 2                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine2(int shift, const double &close[], const double &high[], 
                          const double &low[], const double &ma[], const double &atr[])
{
    static double TrendLine_prev = 0;
    static int iTrend_prev = 0;
    
    // Calculate BB Upper and Lower based on MA
    double sum = 0;
    int count = 0;
    
    for(int i = shift; i < shift + InpMAPeriod && i < ArraySize(close); i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[i] - ma[shift], 2);
        count++;
    }
    double stdev = (count > 0) ? MathSqrt(sum / count) : 0;
    
    double BBUpper = ma[shift] + stdev * InpBBDeviations;
    double BBLower = ma[shift] - stdev * InpBBDeviations;
    
    // Determine BB Signal
    int BBSignal = 0;
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper)
        BBSignal = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower)
        BBSignal = -1;
    
    // Calculate TrendLine
    double TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    double v = atr[shift];
    
    if(BBSignal == 1)
    {
        TrendLine = low[shift] - v;
        if(TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal == -1)
    {
        TrendLine = high[shift] + v;
        if(TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    
    // Determine trend
    int iTrend = iTrend_prev;
    if(TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
        iTrend = 1;
    else if(TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
        iTrend = -1;
    
    // Detect signals
    if(iTrend_prev == -1 && iTrend == 1 && !InpHideLabels2)
        BuySignalBuffer[shift] = TrendLine - v;
    else
        BuySignalBuffer[shift] = EMPTY_VALUE;
    
    if(iTrend_prev == 1 && iTrend == -1 && !InpHideLabels2)
        SellSignalBuffer[shift] = TrendLine + v;
    else
        SellSignalBuffer[shift] = EMPTY_VALUE;
    
    // Update buffer
    TrendLineBuffer[shift] = TrendLine;
    
    // Save for next iteration
    TrendLine_prev = TrendLine;
    iTrend_prev = iTrend;
    
    // Update lastSwingLow and lastSwingHigh for auto lot calculator
    if(iTrend_prev == -1 && iTrend == 1)
    {
        lastSwingLow = TrendLine;
        p1 = close[shift];
        p2_calc = lastSwingLow - 100 * _Point;
        entryBar = shift;
    }
    
    if(iTrend_prev == 1 && iTrend == -1)
    {
        lastSwingHigh = TrendLine;
        p1 = close[shift];
        p2_calc = lastSwingHigh + 100 * _Point;
        entryBar = shift;
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Kernel Regression (Simplified)                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateKernelRegression(int shift, const double &close[])
{
    // Simplified Rational Quadratic Kernel
    double currentWeight = 0;
    double cumulativeWeight = 0;
    
    for(int i = 0; i < InpLookbackWindow && (shift + i) < ArraySize(close); i++)
    {
        double y = close[shift + i];
        double w = MathPow(1 + (MathPow(i, 2) / (MathPow(InpLookbackWindow, 2) * 2 * InpWeighting)), -InpWeighting);
        currentWeight += y * w;
        cumulativeWeight += w;
    }
    
    return (cumulativeWeight != 0) ? currentWeight / cumulativeWeight : close[shift];
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Detect Fractal High                                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool DetectFractalHigh(int shift, const double &high[], string filter)
{
    if(filter == "3")
    {
        // 3-bar fractal
        if(shift < 2) return false;
        return (high[shift] < high[shift+1] && 
                (high[shift+2] < high[shift+1] || 
                 (high[shift+2] == high[shift+1] && shift+3 < ArraySize(high) && high[shift+3] < high[shift+2])));
    }
    else
    {
        // 5-bar fractal (Bill Williams)
        if(shift < 4) return false;
        return (high[shift] < high[shift+2] && 
                high[shift+1] < high[shift+2] && 
                high[shift+3] < high[shift+2] && 
                high[shift+4] < high[shift+2]);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Detect Fractal Low                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool DetectFractalLow(int shift, const double &low[], string filter)
{
    if(filter == "3")
    {
        // 3-bar fractal
        if(shift < 2) return false;
        return (low[shift] > low[shift+1] && 
                (low[shift+2] > low[shift+1] || 
                 (low[shift+2] == low[shift+1] && shift+3 < ArraySize(low) && low[shift+3] > low[shift+2])));
    }
    else
    {
        // 5-bar fractal (Bill Williams)
        if(shift < 4) return false;
        return (low[shift] > low[shift+2] && 
                low[shift+1] > low[shift+2] && 
                low[shift+3] > low[shift+2] && 
                low[shift+4] > low[shift+2]);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Add Fractal High to Array                                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddFractalHigh(double price, datetime t)
{
    int size = ArraySize(fractal_highs);
    ArrayResize(fractal_highs, size + 1);
    ArrayResize(fractal_high_times, size + 1);
    
    fractal_highs[size] = price;
    fractal_high_times[size] = (int)t;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Add Fractal Low to Array                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddFractalLow(double price, datetime t)
{
    int size = ArraySize(fractal_lows);
    ArrayResize(fractal_lows, size + 1);
    ArrayResize(fractal_low_times, size + 1);
    
    fractal_lows[size] = price;
    fractal_low_times[size] = (int)t;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update ZigZag                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateZigZag(const datetime &time[], const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                  const double &close[], int total)
{
    // Simplified ZigZag implementation
    static int lastPivot = 0;
    static double lastPrice = 0;
    static int direction = 0; // 1 = up, -1 = down
    
    for(int i = total - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        bool isHigh = DetectFractalHigh(i, high, InpFractalFilter);
        bool isLow = DetectFractalLow(i, low, InpFractalFilter);
        
        if(isHigh && direction != 1)
        {
            AddZigZagPoint(time[i], high[i], 1, "H");
            direction = 1;
            lastPrice = high[i];
            lastPivot = i;
            lastSwingHigh = high[i];
        }
        
        if(isLow && direction != -1)
        {
            AddZigZagPoint(time[i], low[i], -1, "L");
            direction = -1;
            lastPrice = low[i];
            lastPivot = i;
            lastSwingLow = low[i];
        }
    }
    
    DrawZigZagLines();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Add ZigZag Point                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddZigZagPoint(datetime t, double price, int type, string lbl)
{
    int size = ArraySize(zigzagPoints);
    ArrayResize(zigzagPoints, size + 1);
    
    zigzagPoints[size].time = t;
    zigzagPoints[size].price = price;
    zigzagPoints[size].type = type;
    zigzagPoints[size].label = lbl;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Draw ZigZag Lines                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DrawZigZagLines()
{
    if(!InpShowZigZagLine) return;
    
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "ZZ_LINE_");
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "ZZ_LABEL_");
    
    int size = ArraySize(zigzagPoints);
    
    for(int i = 0; i < size - 1; i++)
    {
        string lineName = "ZZ_LINE_" + IntegerToString(i);
        color lineColor = (zigzagPoints[i].type == 1) ? InpColorUp : InpColorDn;
        
        ObjectCreate(0, lineName, OBJ_TREND, 0, 
                    zigzagPoints[i].time, zigzagPoints[i].price,
                    zigzagPoints[i+1].time, zigzagPoints[i+1].price);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, lineName, OBJPROP_COLOR, lineColor);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, lineName, OBJPROP_WIDTH, InpZigZagWidth);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, lineName, OBJPROP_RAY_RIGHT, false);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, lineName, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
        
        if(InpShowZigZagLabel)
        {
            string labelName = "ZZ_LABEL_" + IntegerToString(i);
            string labelText = (InpDisplayLabel == "HHLL") ? 
                              DetermineHHLL(i) : zigzagPoints[i].label;
            
            ObjectCreate(0, labelName, OBJ_TEXT, 0, zigzagPoints[i].time, zigzagPoints[i].price);
            ObjectSetString(0, labelName, OBJPROP_TEXT, labelText);
            ObjectSetInteger(0, labelName, OBJPROP_COLOR, lineColor);
            ObjectSetInteger(0, labelName, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, 8);
            ObjectSetInteger(0, labelName, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Determine HH/LL/HL/LH                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string DetermineHHLL(int index)
{
    int size = ArraySize(zigzagPoints);
    
    if(index < 2 || index >= size)
        return zigzagPoints[index].label;
    
    if(zigzagPoints[index].type == 1)
    {
        if(zigzagPoints[index].price > zigzagPoints[index-2].price)
            return "HH";
        else
            return "LH";
    }
    else
    {
        if(zigzagPoints[index].price < zigzagPoints[index-2].price)
            return "LL";
        else
            return "HL";
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Order Blocks                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateOrderBlocks(const datetime &time[], const double &open[], const double &high[], 
                       const double &low[], const double &close[], int total)
{
    for(int i = ArraySize(bearOBLines) - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        if(!bearOBLines[i].active) continue;
        
        if(high[0] >= bearOBLines[i].price1)
        {
            if(InpDeleteLines)
            {
                string objName = "OB_BEAR_" + IntegerToString(i);
                ObjectDelete(0, objName + "_1");
                ObjectDelete(0, objName + "_2");
            }
            bearOBLines[i].active = false;
        }
    }
    
    for(int i = ArraySize(bullOBLines) - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        if(!bullOBLines[i].active) continue;
        
        if(low[0] <= bullOBLines[i].price1)
        {
            if(InpDeleteLines)
            {
                string objName = "OB_BULL_" + IntegerToString(i);
                ObjectDelete(0, objName + "_1");
                ObjectDelete(0, objName + "_2");
            }
            bullOBLines[i].active = false;
        }
    }
    
    DetectNewOrderBlocks(time, open, high, low, close, total);
    DrawOrderBlockLines();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Detect New Order Blocks                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DetectNewOrderBlocks(const datetime &time[], const double &open[], const double &high[], 
                          const double &low[], const double &close[], int total)
{
    for(int i = ArraySize(fractal_lows) - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        double fractalPrice = fractal_lows[i];
        bool breakCondition = (InpFindOBType == "Close") ? 
                              (close[0] < fractalPrice) : 
                              (low[0] < fractalPrice);
        
        if(breakCondition)
        {
            int obIndex = FindBullishOB(0, time, open, high, low, close, total, fractal_low_times[i]);
            
            if(obIndex > 0)
            {
                AddBearOB(time[obIndex], high[obIndex], low[obIndex], open[obIndex]);
            }
            
            RemoveFractalLow(i);
        }
    }
    
    for(int i = ArraySize(fractal_highs) - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        double fractalPrice = fractal_highs[i];
        bool breakCondition = (InpFindOBType == "Close") ? 
                              (close[0] > fractalPrice) : 
                              (high[0] > fractalPrice);
        
        if(breakCondition)
        {
            int obIndex = FindBearishOB(0, time, open, high, low, close, total, fractal_high_times[i]);
            
            if(obIndex > 0)
            {
                AddBullOB(time[obIndex], low[obIndex], high[obIndex], open[obIndex]);
            }
            
            RemoveFractalHigh(i);
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Find Bullish OB                                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int FindBullishOB(int start, const datetime &time[], const double &open[], const double &high[], 
                  const double &low[], const double &close[], int total, int fractalTime)
{
    double maxHigh = low[start];
    int obIndex = 0;
    
    for(int k = start; k < MathMin(start + 500, total); k++)
    {
        if(time[k] < fractalTime)
            break;
        
        if(close[k] > open[k] && high[k] > maxHigh)
        {
            obIndex = k;
            maxHigh = high[k];
        }
    }
    
    return obIndex;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Find Bearish OB                                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int FindBearishOB(int start, const datetime &time[], const double &open[], const double &high[], 
                  const double &low[], const double &close[], int total, int fractalTime)
{
    double minLow = high[start];
    int obIndex = 0;
    
    for(int k = start; k < MathMin(start + 500, total); k++)
    {
        if(time[k] < fractalTime)
            break;
        
        if(close[k] < open[k] && low[k] < minLow)
        {
            obIndex = k;
            minLow = low[k];
        }
    }
    
    return obIndex;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Add Bear OB                                                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddBearOB(datetime t, double high, double low, double open)
{
    int size = ArraySize(bearOBLines);
    ArrayResize(bearOBLines, size + 1);
    
    bearOBLines[size].time1 = t;
    bearOBLines[size].price1 = high;
    bearOBLines[size].time2 = t + InpOBLineLength * PeriodSeconds();
    bearOBLines[size].price2 = (InpLineHeight == "Body") ? open : low;
    bearOBLines[size].lineColor = InpBearOBColor;
    bearOBLines[size].active = true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Add Bull OB                                                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AddBullOB(datetime t, double low, double high, double open)
{
    int size = ArraySize(bullOBLines);
    ArrayResize(bullOBLines, size + 1);
    
    bullOBLines[size].time1 = t;
    bullOBLines[size].price1 = low;
    bullOBLines[size].time2 = t + InpOBLineLength * PeriodSeconds();
    bullOBLines[size].price2 = (InpLineHeight == "Body") ? open : high;
    bullOBLines[size].lineColor = InpBullOBColor;
    bullOBLines[size].active = true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Remove Fractal High                                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RemoveFractalHigh(int index)
{
    int size = ArraySize(fractal_highs);
    if(index < 0 || index >= size) return;
    
    for(int i = index; i < size - 1; i++)
    {
        fractal_highs[i] = fractal_highs[i + 1];
        fractal_high_times[i] = fractal_high_times[i + 1];
    }
    
    ArrayResize(fractal_highs, size - 1);
    ArrayResize(fractal_high_times, size - 1);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Remove Fractal Low                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void RemoveFractalLow(int index)
{
    int size = ArraySize(fractal_lows);
    if(index < 0 || index >= size) return;
    
    for(int i = index; i < size - 1; i++)
    {
        fractal_lows[i] = fractal_lows[i + 1];
        fractal_low_times[i] = fractal_low_times[i + 1];
    }
    
    ArrayResize(fractal_lows, size - 1);
    ArrayResize(fractal_low_times, size - 1);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Draw Order Block Lines                                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DrawOrderBlockLines()
{
    for(int i = 0; i < ArraySize(bearOBLines); i++)
    {
        if(!bearOBLines[i].active) continue;
        
        string objName1 = "OB_BEAR_" + IntegerToString(i) + "_1";
        string objName2 = "OB_BEAR_" + IntegerToString(i) + "_2";
        
        ENUM_LINE_STYLE style = (InpOBLineStyle == "Solid") ? STYLE_SOLID : 
                                (InpOBLineStyle == "Dashed") ? STYLE_DASH : STYLE_DOT;
        
        ObjectCreate(0, objName1, OBJ_TREND, 0, 
                    bearOBLines[i].time1, bearOBLines[i].price1,
                    bearOBLines[i].time2, bearOBLines[i].price1);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_COLOR, bearOBLines[i].lineColor);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_WIDTH, InpOBLineWidth);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_STYLE, style);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_RAY_RIGHT, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
        
        ObjectCreate(0, objName2, OBJ_TREND, 0, 
                    bearOBLines[i].time1, bearOBLines[i].price2,
                    bearOBLines[i].time2, bearOBLines[i].price2);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_COLOR, bearOBLines[i].lineColor);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_WIDTH, InpOBLineWidth);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_STYLE, style);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_RAY_RIGHT, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    }
    
    for(int i = 0; i < ArraySize(bullOBLines); i++)
    {
        if(!bullOBLines[i].active) continue;
        
        string objName1 = "OB_BULL_" + IntegerToString(i) + "_1";
        string objName2 = "OB_BULL_" + IntegerToString(i) + "_2";
        
        ENUM_LINE_STYLE style = (InpOBLineStyle == "Solid") ? STYLE_SOLID : 
                                (InpOBLineStyle == "Dashed") ? STYLE_DASH : STYLE_DOT;
        
        ObjectCreate(0, objName1, OBJ_TREND, 0, 
                    bullOBLines[i].time1, bullOBLines[i].price1,
                    bullOBLines[i].time2, bullOBLines[i].price1);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_COLOR, bullOBLines[i].lineColor);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_WIDTH, InpOBLineWidth);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_STYLE, style);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_RAY_RIGHT, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName1, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
        
        ObjectCreate(0, objName2, OBJ_TREND, 0, 
                    bullOBLines[i].time1, bullOBLines[i].price2,
                    bullOBLines[i].time2, bullOBLines[i].price2);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_COLOR, bullOBLines[i].lineColor);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_WIDTH, InpOBLineWidth);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_STYLE, style);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_RAY_RIGHT, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, objName2, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Draw Support & Resistance                                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DrawSupportResistance(const double &high[], const double &low[], const double &close[], int total)
{
    if(!InpEnableSR) return;
    
    double highestPrice = high[ArrayMaximum(high, 0, 300)];
    double lowestPrice = low[ArrayMinimum(low, 0, 300)];
    
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "SR_HIGH_");
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "SR_LOW_");
    
    color hiColor = (close[0] >= highestPrice) ? InpColorSup : InpColorRes;
    ObjectCreate(0, "SR_HIGH_MAIN", OBJ_HLINE, 0, 0, highestPrice);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_MAIN", OBJPROP_COLOR, hiColor);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_MAIN", OBJPROP_WIDTH, InpLineWidth);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_MAIN", OBJPROP_STYLE, InpLineStyle);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_MAIN", OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    
    color loColor = (close[0] >= lowestPrice) ? InpColorSup : InpColorRes;
    ObjectCreate(0, "SR_LOW_MAIN", OBJ_HLINE, 0, 0, lowestPrice);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_MAIN", OBJPROP_COLOR, loColor);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_MAIN", OBJPROP_WIDTH, InpLineWidth);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_MAIN", OBJPROP_STYLE, InpLineStyle);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_MAIN", OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    
    if(InpUseHLZones)
    {
        double zonePercCalc = (highestPrice - lowestPrice) * InpZoneWidth / 100.0;
        
        ObjectCreate(0, "SR_HIGH_ZONE", OBJ_RECTANGLE, 0, 
                    TimeCurrent() - PeriodSeconds() * 311, highestPrice + zonePercCalc,
                    TimeCurrent(), highestPrice - zonePercCalc);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_ZONE", OBJPROP_COLOR, hiColor);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_ZONE", OBJPROP_FILL, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_ZONE", OBJPROP_BACK, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_HIGH_ZONE", OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
        
        ObjectCreate(0, "SR_LOW_ZONE", OBJ_RECTANGLE, 0, 
                    TimeCurrent() - PeriodSeconds() * 311, lowestPrice + zonePercCalc,
                    TimeCurrent(), lowestPrice - zonePercCalc);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_ZONE", OBJPROP_COLOR, loColor);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_ZONE", OBJPROP_FILL, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_ZONE", OBJPROP_BACK, true);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SR_LOW_ZONE", OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Draw Trade Setup (Boxes and Entry Line)                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DrawTradeSetup(double currentClose, datetime currentTime)
{
    if(p1 == 0 || p2_calc == 0) return;
    
    ObjectDelete(0, "TRADE_RISK_BOX");
    ObjectDelete(0, "TRADE_TARGET_BOX");
    ObjectDelete(0, "TRADE_ENTRY_LINE");
    
    datetime startTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, entryBar);
    datetime endTime = TimeCurrent() + InpBoxExtendBars * PeriodSeconds();
    
    ObjectCreate(0, "TRADE_RISK_BOX", OBJ_RECTANGLE, 0, startTime, p1, endTime, p2_calc);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_RISK_BOX", OBJPROP_COLOR, InpRiskBoxColor);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_RISK_BOX", OBJPROP_FILL, true);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_RISK_BOX", OBJPROP_BACK, true);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_RISK_BOX", OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    
    double riskval = MathAbs(p1 - p2_calc);
    if(p1 > p2_calc)
        targetVal = p1 + (riskval * MathAbs(InpRiskReward));
    else
        targetVal = p1 - (riskval * MathAbs(InpRiskReward));
    
    ObjectCreate(0, "TRADE_TARGET_BOX", OBJ_RECTANGLE, 0, startTime, p1, endTime, targetVal);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_TARGET_BOX", OBJPROP_COLOR, InpTargetBoxColor);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_TARGET_BOX", OBJPROP_FILL, true);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_TARGET_BOX", OBJPROP_BACK, true);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_TARGET_BOX", OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    
    if(InpShowEntry)
    {
        ObjectCreate(0, "TRADE_ENTRY_LINE", OBJ_HLINE, 0, 0, p1);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_ENTRY_LINE", OBJPROP_COLOR, clrOrange);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_ENTRY_LINE", OBJPROP_WIDTH, 1);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_ENTRY_LINE", OBJPROP_STYLE, STYLE_SOLID);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "TRADE_ENTRY_LINE", OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Draw Information Table                                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DrawTable()
{
    if(p1 == 0 || p2_calc == 0) return;
    
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "TABLE_");
    
    double riskval = MathAbs(p1 - p2_calc);
    double riskcent_calc = MathAbs(InpRiskPercent) / 100.0;
    double risk = InpBalance * riskcent_calc;
    
    double vt = 1.0;
    string accountCur = AccountInfoString(ACCOUNT_CURRENCY);
    bool needConversion = (accountCur != InpAccountCur);
    
    if(needConversion)
    {
        string pair = InpAccountCur + accountCur;
        vt = SymbolInfoDouble(pair, SYMBOL_BID);
        if(vt == 0) vt = 1.0 / SymbolInfoDouble(accountCur + InpAccountCur, SYMBOL_BID);
    }
    
    double DperP = risk / (riskval * InpUnitsLot);
    if(needConversion)
        DperP = DperP * vt;
    
    double lots = NormalizeDouble(MathAbs(DperP), 2);
    
    int x_start = 20;
    int y_start = 50;
    int x_label = x_start;
    int x_value = x_start + 120;
    int y_pos = y_start;
    int y_step = 18;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_HEADER", x_start, y_pos, "═══ TRADE INFO ═══", InpDashTextCol, InpDashTitleCol, InpTableCorner, 10);
    y_pos += y_step + 5;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_BAL_LABEL", x_label, y_pos, "Balance", InpDashTextCol, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_BAL_VALUE", x_value, y_pos, DoubleToString(InpBalance, 2) + " " + InpAccountCur, clrLightGray, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    y_pos += y_step;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_RISK_LABEL", x_label, y_pos, "Risk", InpDashTextCol, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_RISK_VALUE", x_value, y_pos, DoubleToString(InpRiskPercent, 1) + "%", clrLightGray, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    y_pos += y_step;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_$RISK_LABEL", x_label, y_pos, "$ Risk", InpDashTextCol, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_$RISK_VALUE", x_value, y_pos, DoubleToString(risk, 2), clrLightGray, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    y_pos += y_step;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_ENTRY_LABEL", x_label, y_pos, "Entry", InpDashTextCol, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_ENTRY_VALUE", x_value, y_pos, DoubleToString(p1, _Digits), clrOrange, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    y_pos += y_step;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_SL_LABEL", x_label, y_pos, "Stop Loss", InpDashTextCol, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_SL_VALUE", x_value, y_pos, DoubleToString(p2_calc, _Digits), clrRed, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    y_pos += y_step;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_TP_LABEL", x_label, y_pos, "Take Profit", InpDashTextCol, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_TP_VALUE", x_value, y_pos, DoubleToString(targetVal, _Digits), clrLime, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    y_pos += y_step;
    
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_LOTS_LABEL", x_label, y_pos, "Lots", InpDashTextCol, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
    CreateTableLabel("TABLE_LOTS_VALUE", x_value, y_pos, DoubleToString(lots, 2), clrLimeGreen, InpDashDataCol, InpTableCorner);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Create Table Label                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateTableLabel(string name, int x, int y, string text, color textColor, color bgColor, 
                      ENUM_BASE_CORNER corner, int fontSize = 8)
{
    ObjectCreate(0, name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_CORNER, corner);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, x);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, y);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_TEXT, text);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_COLOR, textColor);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, fontSize);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Arial Bold");
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

---

## ✅ สิ่งที่แก้ไขแล้ว:

1. **ลบ #include ที่ไม่จำเป็น** - ArrayObj.mqh และ ArrayDouble.mqh
2. **แก้ไขฟังก์ชัน iSAR** - เปลี่ยนจาก 5 parameters เป็น 4 parameters
3. **เพิ่ม SAR handle** - สร้าง handle และใช้ CopyBuffer แทน
4. **เพิ่ม input parameters

----- [user] -----
จาก indicator ที่ทำ ทำให้เป็น EA ซื้อ ขาย อัตโนมัติ

----- [assistant] -----
<details type="reasoning" done="true" duration="5">
<summary>Thought for 5 seconds</summary>
&gt; ผู้ใช้ต้องการให้แปลง Indicator ที่สร้างไว้ให้เป็น Expert Advisor (EA) ที่สามารถซื้อขายอัตโนมัติได้
&gt; 
&gt; ฟีเจอร์หลักที่ต้องมี:
&gt; 1. ใช้ Signal จาก Indicator (Buy/Sell signals)
&gt; 2. เปิด Order อัตโนมัติ
&gt; 3. ตั้ง Stop Loss และ Take Profit ตาม calculation
&gt; 4. Money Management (Lot size calculation)
&gt; 5. Trailing Stop (Optional)
&gt; 6. Risk Management
&gt; 7. Order Management (ปิด order เมื่อมี signal ตรงข้าม)
&gt; 
&gt; จะสร้าง EA ที่:
&gt; - ใช้ signals จาก Follow Line และ Smart Trail
&gt; - คำนวณ lot size อัตโนมัติ
&gt; - ตั้ง SL/TP ตาม ZigZag swing points
&gt; - มี Trailing Stop
&gt; - มี Risk Management
&gt; - มี Dashboard แสดงข้อมูล
</details>
# 🤖 สร้าง EA (Expert Advisor) แบบอัตโนมัติ

ผมจะสร้าง EA ที่ใช้ signal จาก Indicator ที่สร้างไว้ พร้อม features ครบครัน:

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                                      Geno_Smart_EA_V23.mq5       |
//|                        Auto Trading Based on Smart Signals       |
//|                                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Geno Smart EA V2.3"
#property link      ""
#property version   "2.30"
#property strict

#include <Trade\Trade.mqh>
#include <Trade\PositionInfo.mqh>
#include <Trade\AccountInfo.mqh>

CTrade trade;
CPositionInfo position;
CAccountInfo account;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - EA Settings                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ EA SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpAutoTrading = true;              // Enable Auto Trading
input int     InpMagicNumber = 123456;            // Magic Number
input string  InpTradeComment = "Geno_Smart_V23"; // Trade Comment
input bool    InpOnlyOneTradePerSignal = true;    // Only One Trade Per Signal
input int     InpMaxPositions = 1;                // Max Open Positions
input bool    InpCloseOppositeSignal = true;      // Close on Opposite Signal

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Smart Trail Settings                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ SIGNAL SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpUseSmartTrail = true;            // Use Smart Trail Signals
input bool    InpUseFollowLine1 = true;           // Use Follow Line 1 Signals
input bool    InpUseFollowLine2 = true;           // Use Follow Line 2 Signals
input bool    InpUseKernel = false;               // Use Kernel Regression
input int     InpMinSignalConfirmation = 2;       // Min Signal Confirmation (1-3)

input group "════════════ TRAIL CALCULATION ════════════"
input bool    InpModifiedTrail = true;            // Modified Trail
input int     InpATRPeriod = 200;                 // ATR Period
input double  InpATRFactor = 4.2;                 // ATR Factor (2-26)
input int     InpSmoothing = 4;                   // Smoothing

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Money Management                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ MONEY MANAGEMENT ════════════"
input bool    InpUseAutoLotSize = true;           // Use Auto Lot Calculation
input double  InpFixedLotSize = 0.01;             // Fixed Lot Size
input double  InpRiskPercent = 1.0;               // Risk % per Trade
input double  InpMaxLotSize = 10.0;               // Maximum Lot Size
input double  InpMinLotSize = 0.01;               // Minimum Lot Size
input int     InpLotDigits = 2;                   // Lot Size Digits

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Stop Loss & Take Profit                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ STOP LOSS & TAKE PROFIT ════════════"
input bool    InpUseDynamicSL = true;             // Use Dynamic SL (Swing Points)
input int     InpFixedSLPoints = 500;             // Fixed SL in Points
input bool    InpUseDynamicTP = true;             // Use Dynamic TP (Risk:Reward)
input double  InpRiskRewardRatio = 2.0;           // Risk:Reward Ratio
input int     InpFixedTPPoints = 1000;            // Fixed TP in Points
input int     InpMinSLPoints = 100;               // Minimum SL in Points
input int     InpMaxSLPoints = 3000;              // Maximum SL in Points
input int     InpSLBuffer = 100;                  // SL Buffer in Points

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Trailing Stop                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ TRAILING STOP ════════════"
input bool    InpUseTrailingStop = true;          // Enable Trailing Stop
input int     InpTrailingStart = 200;             // Trailing Start (Points)
input int     InpTrailingStep = 100;              // Trailing Step (Points)
input bool    InpTrailingFollowLine = true;       // Trail Follow Line

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Time Filter                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ TIME FILTER ════════════"
input bool    InpUseTimeFilter = false;           // Enable Time Filter
input int     InpStartHour = 0;                   // Start Hour (0-23)
input int     InpEndHour = 23;                    // End Hour (0-23)
input bool    InpTradeMonday = true;              // Trade on Monday
input bool    InpTradeTuesday = true;             // Trade on Tuesday
input bool    InpTradeWednesday = true;           // Trade on Wednesday
input bool    InpTradeThursday = true;            // Trade on Thursday
input bool    InpTradeFriday = true;              // Trade on Friday

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Follow Line Settings                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ FOLLOW LINE 1 ════════════"
input int     InpBBPeriod1 = 21;                  // BB Period
input double  InpBBDeviation1 = 1.0;              // BB Deviations
input bool    InpUseATRFilter1 = true;            // Use ATR Filter
input int     InpATRPeriod1 = 5;                  // ATR Period

input group "════════════ FOLLOW LINE 2 (MA) ════════════"
input bool    InpUseCurrentTF = true;             // Use Current Timeframe
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES InpMATF = PERIOD_H1;        // MA Timeframe
input int     InpMAPeriod = 21;                   // MA Period
input ENUM_MA_METHOD InpMAMethod = MODE_EMA;      // MA Method
input double  InpBBDeviations = 1.0;              // BB Deviations
input int     InpATRPeriod2 = 5;                  // ATR Period

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Dashboard                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ DASHBOARD ════════════"
input bool    InpShowDashboard = true;            // Show Dashboard
input ENUM_BASE_CORNER InpDashCorner = CORNER_LEFT_UPPER; // Dashboard Corner
input color   InpDashTitleCol = clrDodgerBlue;    // Title Color
input color   InpDashTextCol = clrWhite;          // Text Color
input color   InpDashBgCol = clrBlack;            // Background Color

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Global Variables                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
// Indicator Handles
int atrHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;
int atrHandle1 = INVALID_HANDLE;
int atrHandle2 = INVALID_HANDLE;
int maHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;

// Signal Variables
bool g_buySignal = false;
bool g_sellSignal = false;
bool g_lastTradeWasBuy = false;
bool g_lastTradeWasSell = false;
datetime g_lastBarTime = 0;
int g_signalConfirmCount = 0;
int g_lastSignalType = 0; // 1 = buy, -1 = sell, 0 = none

// Trade Management
double g_lastSwingHigh = 0;
double g_lastSwingLow = 0;
double g_currentTrailStop = 0;
double g_entryPrice = 0;
datetime g_lastTradeTime = 0;

// Trail Calculation Buffers
double g_trailBuffer[];
double g_trendUpBuffer[];
double g_trendDownBuffer[];
double g_trendLine1Buffer[];
double g_trendLineBuffer[];

// Statistics
int g_totalTrades = 0;
int g_winTrades = 0;
int g_loseTrades = 0;
double g_totalProfit = 0;
double g_totalLoss = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert initialization function                                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
    // Set Magic Number
    trade.SetExpertMagicNumber(InpMagicNumber);
    trade.SetDeviationInPoints(50);
    trade.SetTypeFilling(ORDER_FILLING_FOK);
    trade.SetAsyncMode(false);
    
    // Initialize indicator handles
    atrHandle = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod);
    atrHandle1 = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod1);
    atrHandle2 = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod2);
    
    ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf = InpUseCurrentTF ? PERIOD_CURRENT : InpMATF;
    maHandle = iMA(_Symbol, tf, InpMAPeriod, 0, InpMAMethod, PRICE_CLOSE);
    
    if(atrHandle == INVALID_HANDLE || atrHandle1 == INVALID_HANDLE || 
       atrHandle2 == INVALID_HANDLE || maHandle == INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        Print("Error creating indicator handles");
        return(INIT_FAILED);
    }
    
    // Initialize buffers
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trailBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendUpBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendDownBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendLine1Buffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendLineBuffer, true);
    
    ArrayResize(g_trailBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendUpBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendDownBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendLine1Buffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendLineBuffer, 500);
    
    // Load statistics
    LoadStatistics();
    
    // Show initial dashboard
    if(InpShowDashboard)
        UpdateDashboard();
    
    Print("Geno Smart EA V2.3 initialized successfully");
    Print("Auto Trading: ", InpAutoTrading ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED");
    Print("Magic Number: ", InpMagicNumber);
    
    return(INIT_SUCCEEDED);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert deinitialization function                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
    // Release indicator handles
    if(atrHandle != INVALID_HANDLE)
        IndicatorRelease(atrHandle);
    if(atrHandle1 != INVALID_HANDLE)
        IndicatorRelease(atrHandle1);
    if(atrHandle2 != INVALID_HANDLE)
        IndicatorRelease(atrHandle2);
    if(maHandle != INVALID_HANDLE)
        IndicatorRelease(maHandle);
    
    // Delete dashboard
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    // Save statistics
    SaveStatistics();
    
    Comment("");
    Print("Geno Smart EA V2.3 deinitialized");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert tick function                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
    // Check if new bar
    if(!IsNewBar())
        return;
    
    // Check if auto trading is enabled
    if(!InpAutoTrading)
    {
        if(InpShowDashboard)
            UpdateDashboard();
        return;
    }
    
    // Check time filter
    if(InpUseTimeFilter && !CheckTimeFilter())
        return;
    
    // Calculate indicators
    CalculateIndicators();
    
    // Check for signals
    CheckForSignals();
    
    // Manage open positions
    ManagePositions();
    
    // Update dashboard
    if(InpShowDashboard)
        UpdateDashboard();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if new bar                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsNewBar()
{
    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    
    if(g_lastBarTime != currentBarTime)
    {
        g_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
        return true;
    }
    
    return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check time filter                                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CheckTimeFilter()
{
    MqlDateTime dt;
    TimeToStruct(TimeCurrent(), dt);
    
    // Check hour
    if(dt.hour < InpStartHour || dt.hour >= InpEndHour)
        return false;
    
    // Check day of week
    switch(dt.day_of_week)
    {
        case 1: if(!InpTradeMonday) return false; break;
        case 2: if(!InpTradeTuesday) return false; break;
        case 3: if(!InpTradeWednesday) return false; break;
        case 4: if(!InpTradeThursday) return false; break;
        case 5: if(!InpTradeFriday) return false; break;
        default: return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Indicators                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicators()
{
    // Get price data
    double high[], low[], close[], open[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(high, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(low, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(close, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(open, true);
    
    int bars = 100;
    CopyHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, high);
    CopyLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, low);
    CopyClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, close);
    CopyOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, open);
    
    // Get ATR values
    double atr[], atr1[], atr2[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr1, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr2, true);
    
    CopyBuffer(atrHandle, 0, 0, bars, atr);
    CopyBuffer(atrHandle1, 0, 0, bars, atr1);
    CopyBuffer(atrHandle2, 0, 0, bars, atr2);
    
    // Get MA values
    double ma[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(ma, true);
    CopyBuffer(maHandle, 0, 0, bars, ma);
    
    // Calculate Smart Trail
    if(InpUseSmartTrail)
        CalculateSmartTrail(high, low, close, atr);
    
    // Calculate Follow Line 1
    if(InpUseFollowLine1)
        CalculateFollowLine1(high, low, close, atr1);
    
    // Calculate Follow Line 2
    if(InpUseFollowLine2)
        CalculateFollowLine2(high, low, close, ma, atr2);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Smart Trail                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateSmartTrail(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                         const double &close[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    // Calculate True Range
    double trueRange = CalculateTrueRange(shift, high, low, close);
    
    // Calculate Wild MA
    static double wild_prev = 0;
    double wild = (wild_prev == 0) ? trueRange : wild_prev + (trueRange - wild_prev) / InpATRPeriod;
    wild_prev = wild;
    
    double loss = InpATRFactor * wild;
    
    // Calculate Up and Down levels
    double Up68 = close[shift] - loss;
    double Dn68 = close[shift] + loss;
    
    // Calculate Trend
    if(shift == 0 && g_trendUpBuffer[1] != 0)
    {
        if(close[1] > g_trendUpBuffer[1])
            g_trendUpBuffer[0] = MathMax(Up68, g_trendUpBuffer[1]);
        else
            g_trendUpBuffer[0] = Up68;
        
        if(close[1] < g_trendDownBuffer[1])
            g_trendDownBuffer[0] = MathMin(Dn68, g_trendDownBuffer[1]);
        else
            g_trendDownBuffer[0] = Dn68;
    }
    else
    {
        g_trendUpBuffer[0] = Up68;
        g_trendDownBuffer[0] = Dn68;
    }
    
    // Determine trend
    int trend = 0;
    if(close[0] > g_trendDownBuffer[1])
        trend = 1;
    else if(close[0] < g_trendUpBuffer[1])
        trend = -1;
    else
        trend = (g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendUpBuffer[1]) ? 1 : -1;
    
    g_trailBuffer[0] = (trend == 1) ? g_trendUpBuffer[0] : g_trendDownBuffer[0];
    
    // Update swing points
    if(trend == 1)
        g_lastSwingLow = g_trailBuffer[0];
    else
        g_lastSwingHigh = g_trailBuffer[0];
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate True Range                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateTrueRange(int shift, const double &high[], 
                          const double &low[], const double &close[])
{
    if(shift >= ArraySize(high) - 1)
        return 0;
    
    double tr1 = high[shift] - low[shift];
    double tr2 = MathAbs(high[shift] - close[shift+1]);
    double tr3 = MathAbs(low[shift] - close[shift+1]);
    
    return MathMax(tr1, MathMax(tr2, tr3));
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 1                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine1(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                          const double &close[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    // Calculate BB
    double sma = 0;
    double sum = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < InpBBPeriod1; i++)
    {
        sma += close[shift + i];
    }
    sma /= InpBBPeriod1;
    
    for(int i = 0; i < InpBBPeriod1; i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[shift + i] - sma, 2);
    }
    double stdev = MathSqrt(sum / InpBBPeriod1);
    
    double BBUpper = sma + stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    double BBLower = sma - stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    
    // Calculate TrendLine
    static double TrendLine1_prev = 0;
    static int iTrend1_prev = 0;
    
    double TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    int BBSignal = 0;
    
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper)
        BBSignal = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower)
        BBSignal = -1;
    
    if(BBSignal == 1)
    {
        TrendLine1 = InpUseATRFilter1 ? (low[shift] - atr[shift]) : low[shift];
        if(TrendLine1 < TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal == -1)
    {
        TrendLine1 = InpUseATRFilter1 ? (high[shift] + atr[shift]) : high[shift];
        if(TrendLine1 > TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    
    int iTrend1 = iTrend1_prev;
    if(TrendLine1 > TrendLine1_prev)
        iTrend1 = 1;
    else if(TrendLine1 < TrendLine1_prev)
        iTrend1 = -1;
    
    g_trendLine1Buffer[0] = TrendLine1;
    
    TrendLine1_prev = TrendLine1;
    iTrend1_prev = iTrend1;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 2                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine2(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                          const double &close[], const double &ma[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    // Calculate BB based on MA
    double sum = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < InpMAPeriod; i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[shift + i] - ma[shift], 2);
    }
    double stdev = MathSqrt(sum / InpMAPeriod);
    
    double BBUpper = ma[shift] + stdev * InpBBDeviations;
    double BBLower = ma[shift] - stdev * InpBBDeviations;
    
    // Calculate TrendLine
    static double TrendLine_prev = 0;
    static int iTrend_prev = 0;
    
    double TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    int BBSignal = 0;
    
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper)
        BBSignal = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower)
        BBSignal = -1;
    
    double v = atr[shift];
    
    if(BBSignal == 1)
    {
        TrendLine = low[shift] - v;
        if(TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal == -1)
    {
        TrendLine = high[shift] + v;
        if(TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    
    int iTrend = iTrend_prev;
    if(TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
        iTrend = 1;
    else if(TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
        iTrend = -1;
    
    g_trendLineBuffer[0] = TrendLine;
    
    // Update swing points
    if(iTrend_prev == -1 && iTrend == 1)
        g_lastSwingLow = TrendLine;
    
    if(iTrend_prev == 1 && iTrend == -1)
        g_lastSwingHigh = TrendLine;
    
    TrendLine_prev = TrendLine;
    iTrend_prev = iTrend;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check For Trading Signals                                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForSignals()
{
    g_buySignal = false;
    g_sellSignal = false;
    
    int signalCount = 0;
    int buyCount = 0;
    int sellCount = 0;
    
    // Check Smart Trail Signal
    if(InpUseSmartTrail && g_trailBuffer[1] != 0 && g_trailBuffer[2] != 0)
    {
        // Buy signal: Trail changes from down to up
        if(g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendUpBuffer[1] && g_trailBuffer[2] == g_trendDownBuffer[2])
        {
            buyCount++;
        }
        // Sell signal: Trail changes from up to down
        else if(g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendDownBuffer[1] && g_trailBuffer[2] == g_trendUpBuffer[2])
        {
            sellCount++;
        }
        signalCount++;
    }
    
    // Check Follow Line 1 Signal
    if(InpUseFollowLine1 && g_trendLine1Buffer[1] != 0 && g_trendLine1Buffer[2] != 0)
    {
        if(g_trendLine1Buffer[1] > g_trendLine1Buffer[2])
            buyCount++;
        else if(g_trendLine1Buffer[1] < g_trendLine1Buffer[2])
            sellCount++;
        signalCount++;
    }
    
    // Check Follow Line 2 Signal
    if(InpUseFollowLine2 && g_trendLineBuffer[1] != 0 && g_trendLineBuffer[2] != 0)
    {
        if(g_trendLineBuffer[1] > g_trendLineBuffer[2])
            buyCount++;
        else if(g_trendLineBuffer[1] < g_trendLineBuffer[2])
            sellCount++;
        signalCount++;
    }
    
    // Determine final signal based on confirmation count
    if(buyCount >= InpMinSignalConfirmation)
    {
        g_buySignal = true;
        g_lastSignalType = 1;
    }
    
    if(sellCount >= InpMinSignalConfirmation)
    {
        g_sellSignal = true;
        g_lastSignalType = -1;
    }
    
    // Execute trades
    if(g_buySignal)
    {
        if(InpCloseOppositeSignal)
            ClosePositions(POSITION_TYPE_SELL);
        
        if(CanOpenNewTrade(ORDER_TYPE_BUY))
            OpenBuyPosition();
    }
    
    if(g_sellSignal)
    {
        if(InpCloseOppositeSignal)
            ClosePositions(POSITION_TYPE_BUY);
        
        if(CanOpenNewTrade(ORDER_TYPE_SELL))
            OpenSellPosition();
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Can Open New Trade                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CanOpenNewTrade(ENUM_ORDER_TYPE orderType)
{
    // Check max positions
    if(CountOpenPositions() >= InpMaxPositions)
        return false;
    
    // Check one trade per signal
    if(InpOnlyOneTradePerSignal)
    {
        if(orderType == ORDER_TYPE_BUY && g_lastTradeWasBuy)
            return false;
        if(orderType == ORDER_TYPE_SELL && g_lastTradeWasSell)
            return false;
    }
    
    // Check minimum time between trades (1 minute)
    if(TimeCurrent() - g_lastTradeTime < 60)
        return false;
    
    return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Count Open Positions                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CountOpenPositions()
{
    int count = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        if(position.SelectByIndex(i))
        {
            if(position.Symbol() == _Symbol && position.Magic() == InpMagicNumber)
                count++;
        }
    }
    
    return count;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Buy Position                                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenBuyPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
    double sl = 0, tp = 0;
    
    // Calculate SL
    if(InpUseDynamicSL && g_lastSwingLow > 0)
    {
        sl = g_lastSwingLow - (InpSLBuffer * _Point);
        
        // Validate SL
        double slDistance = price - sl;
        if(slDistance < InpMinSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price - (InpMinSLPoints * _Point);
        if(slDistance > InpMaxSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price - (InpMaxSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    else
    {
        sl = price - (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Calculate TP
    if(InpUseDynamicTP)
    {
        double slDistance = price - sl;
        tp = price + (slDistance * InpRiskRewardRatio);
    }
    else
    {
        tp = price + (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Normalize SL and TP
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    // Calculate lot size
    double lotSize = InpUseAutoLotSize ? 
                     CalculateLotSize(price, sl) : InpFixedLotSize;
    
    lotSize = NormalizeLotSize(lotSize);
    
    // Open position
    if(trade.Buy(lotSize, _Symbol, price, sl, tp, InpTradeComment))
    {
        g_lastTradeWasBuy = true;
        g_lastTradeWasSell = false;
        g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
        g_entryPrice = price;
        g_totalTrades++;
        
        Print("BUY position opened: ", trade.ResultOrder(), 
              " | Price: ", price, 
              " | SL: ", sl, 
              " | TP: ", tp, 
              " | Lots: ", lotSize);
    }
    else
    {
        Print("Error opening BUY position: ", GetLastError());
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Sell Position                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenSellPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    double sl = 0, tp = 0;
    
    // Calculate SL
    if(InpUseDynamicSL && g_lastSwingHigh > 0)
    {
        sl = g_lastSwingHigh + (InpSLBuffer * _Point);
        
        // Validate SL
        double slDistance = sl - price;
        if(slDistance < InpMinSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price + (InpMinSLPoints * _Point);
        if(slDistance > InpMaxSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price + (InpMaxSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    else
    {
        sl = price + (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Calculate TP
    if(InpUseDynamicTP)
    {
        double slDistance = sl - price;
        tp = price - (slDistance * InpRiskRewardRatio);
    }
    else
    {
        tp = price - (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Normalize SL and TP
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    // Calculate lot size
    double lotSize = InpUseAutoLotSize ? 
                     CalculateLotSize(price, sl) : InpFixedLotSize;
    
    lotSize = NormalizeLotSize(lotSize);
    
    // Open position
    if(trade.Sell(lotSize, _Symbol, price, sl, tp, InpTradeComment))
    {
        g_lastTradeWasSell = true;
        g_lastTradeWasBuy = false;
        g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
        g_entryPrice = price;
        g_totalTrades++;
        
        Print("SELL position opened: ", trade.ResultOrder(), 
              " | Price: ", price, 
              " | SL: ", sl, 
              " | TP: ", tp, 
              " | Lots: ", lotSize);
    }
    else
    {
        Print("Error opening SELL position: ", GetLastError());
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Lot Size                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateLotSize(double entryPrice, double stopLoss)
{
    double riskAmount = account.Balance() * (InpRiskPercent / 100.0);
    double slDistance = MathAbs(entryPrice - stopLoss);
    
    if(slDistance == 0)
        return InpMinLotSize;
    
    double tickValue = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
    double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
    
    double lotSize = (riskAmount * tickSize) / (slDistance * tickValue);
    
    return lotSize;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Normalize Lot Size                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double NormalizeLotSize(double lotSize)
{
    double minLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
    double maxLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
    double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
    
    lotSize = MathMax(lotSize, InpMinLotSize);
    lotSize = MathMin(lotSize, InpMaxLotSize);
    lotSize = MathMax(lotSize, minLot);
    lotSize = MathMin(lotSize, maxLot);
    
    lotSize = MathRound(lotSize / lotStep) * lotStep;
    
    return NormalizeDouble(lotSize, InpLotDigits);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Manage Open Positions                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManagePositions()
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        if(!position.SelectByIndex(i))
            continue;
        
        if(position.Symbol() != _Symbol || position.Magic() != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        // Apply trailing stop
        if(InpUseTrailingStop)
            ApplyTrailingStop();
        
        // Check if position is closed
        if(!PositionSelect(position.Ticket()))
        {
            UpdateStatistics(position.Ticket());
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Apply Trailing Stop                                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ApplyTrailingStop()
{
    double currentSL = position.StopLoss();
    double currentTP = position.TakeProfit();
    double newSL = currentSL;
    
    if(position.Type() == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
    {
        double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
        double profit = currentPrice - position.PriceOpen();
        
        if(profit >= InpTrailingStart * _Point)
        {
            if(InpTrailingFollowLine && g_trailBuffer[0] > 0)
            {
                newSL = g_trailBuffer[0];
            }
            else
            {
                newSL = currentPrice - (InpTrailingStep * _Point);
            }
            
            if(newSL > currentSL)
            {
                newSL = NormalizeDouble(newSL, _Digits);
                trade.PositionModify(position.Ticket(), newSL, currentTP);
            }
        }
    }
    else if(position.Type() == POSITION_TYPE_SELL)
    {
        double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
        double profit = position.PriceOpen() - currentPrice;
        
        if(profit >= InpTrailingStart * _Point)
        {
            if(InpTrailingFollowLine && g_trailBuffer[0] > 0)
            {
                newSL = g_trailBuffer[0];
            }
            else
            {
                newSL = currentPrice + (InpTrailingStep * _Point);
            }
            
            if(newSL < currentSL || currentSL == 0)
            {
                newSL = NormalizeDouble(newSL, _Digits);
                trade.PositionModify(position.Ticket(), newSL, currentTP);
            }
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Close Positions                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ClosePositions(ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type)
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        if(!position.SelectByIndex(i))
            continue;
        
        if(position.Symbol() != _Symbol || position.Magic() != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        if(position.Type() == type)
        {
            trade.PositionClose(position.Ticket());
            Print("Position closed due to opposite signal: ", position.Ticket());
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Statistics                                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateStatistics(ulong ticket)
{
    // Get position history
    if(!HistorySelectByPosition(PositionGetInteger(POSITION_IDENTIFIER)))
        return;
    
    double profit = 0;
    
    for(int i = 0; i < HistoryDealsTotal(); i++)
    {
        ulong dealTicket = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
        if(dealTicket > 0)
        {
            profit += HistoryDealGetDouble(dealTicket, DEAL_PROFIT);
        }
    }
    
    if(profit > 0)
    {
        g_winTrades++;
        g_totalProfit += profit;
    }
    else if(profit < 0)
    {
        g_loseTrades++;
        g_totalLoss += MathAbs(profit);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Dashboard                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateDashboard()
{
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    int x = 20;
    int y = 30;
    int lineHeight = 18;
    
    // Title
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE", x, y, "═══ GENO SMART EA V2.3 ═══", InpDashTitleCol, 10);
    y += lineHeight + 5;
    
    // Auto Trading Status
    string status = InpAutoTrading ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED";
    color statusColor = InpAutoTrading ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATUS", x, y, "Auto Trading: " + status, statusColor, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Current Signal
    string signal = g_buySignal ? "BUY" : g_sellSignal ? "SELL" : "WAIT";
    color signalColor = g_buySignal ? clrLime : g_sellSignal ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_SIGNAL", x, y, "Signal: " + signal, signalColor, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Open Positions
    int openPos = CountOpenPositions();
    CreateLabel("DASH_POSITIONS", x, y, "Open Positions: " + IntegerToString(openPos), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Current Profit
    double currentProfit = CalculateCurrentProfit();
    color profitColor = currentProfit >= 0 ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_PROFIT", x, y, "Current P/L: " + DoubleToString(currentProfit, 2), profitColor, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Statistics
    y += 5;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATS_TITLE", x, y, "──── Statistics ────", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_TOTAL_TRADES", x, y, "Total Trades: " + IntegerToString(g_totalTrades), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_WIN_TRADES", x, y, "Win: " + IntegerToString(g_winTrades), clrLime, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_LOSE_TRADES", x, y, "Loss: " + IntegerToString(g_loseTrades), clrRed, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    double winRate = (g_totalTrades > 0) ? (double)g_winTrades / g_totalTrades * 100 : 0;
    CreateLabel("DASH_WINRATE", x, y, "Win Rate: " + DoubleToString(winRate, 1) + "%", InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    double netProfit = g_totalProfit - g_totalLoss;
    color netColor = netProfit >= 0 ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_NET_PROFIT", x, y, "Net Profit: " + DoubleToString(netProfit, 2), netColor, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Account Info
    y += 5;
    CreateLabel("DASH_ACC_TITLE", x, y, "──── Account ────", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_BALANCE", x, y, "Balance: " + DoubleToString(account.Balance(), 2), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_EQUITY", x, y, "Equity: " + DoubleToString(account.Equity(), 2), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Swing Points
    y += 5;
    CreateLabel("DASH_SWING_TITLE", x, y, "──── Swing Points ────", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_SWING_HIGH", x, y, "High: " + DoubleToString(g_lastSwingHigh, _Digits), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_SWING_LOW", x, y, "Low: " + DoubleToString(g_lastSwingLow, _Digits), InpDashTextCol, 8);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Create Label                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateLabel(string name, int x, int y, string text, color clr, int size = 8)
{
    ObjectCreate(0, name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_CORNER, InpDashCorner);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, x);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, y);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_TEXT, text);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clr);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, size);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Arial Bold");
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Current Profit                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateCurrentProfit()
{
    double profit = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        if(position.SelectByIndex(i))
        {
            if(position.Symbol() == _Symbol && position.Magic() == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                profit += position.Profit() + position.Swap();
            }
        }
    }
    
    return profit;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Save Statistics                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void SaveStatistics()
{
    int fileHandle = FileOpen("Geno_EA_Stats_" + IntegerToString(InpMagicNumber) + ".csv", 
                              FILE_WRITE|FILE_CSV);
    
    if(fileHandle != INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        FileWrite(fileHandle, "Total Trades", g_totalTrades);
        FileWrite(fileHandle, "Win Trades", g_winTrades);
        FileWrite(fileHandle, "Lose Trades", g_loseTrades);
        FileWrite(fileHandle, "Total Profit", g_totalProfit);
        FileWrite(fileHandle, "Total Loss", g_totalLoss);
        
        FileClose(fileHandle);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Load Statistics                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void LoadStatistics()
{
    int fileHandle = FileOpen("Geno_EA_Stats_" + IntegerToString(InpMagicNumber) + ".csv", 
                              FILE_READ|FILE_CSV);
    
    if(fileHandle != INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        string temp;
        
        temp = FileReadString(fileHandle);
        g_totalTrades = (int)FileReadNumber(fileHandle);
        
        temp = FileReadString(fileHandle);
        g_winTrades = (int)FileReadNumber(fileHandle);
        
        temp = FileReadString(fileHandle);
        g_loseTrades = (int)FileReadNumber(fileHandle);
        
        temp = FileReadString(fileHandle);
        g_totalProfit = FileReadNumber(fileHandle);
        
        temp = FileReadString(fileHandle);
        g_totalLoss = FileReadNumber(fileHandle);
        
        FileClose(fileHandle);
    }
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

---

## 🎯 **Features ของ EA:**

### ✅ **1. Signal System:**
- ✔️ ใช้ Smart Trail signals
- ✔️ ใช้ Follow Line 1 & 2 signals
- ✔️ Signal confirmation (ต้องมีสัญญาณตรงกันจากหลายตัว)
- ✔️ ปิดออเดอร์ตรงข้ามอัตโนมัติ

### ✅ **2. Money Management:**
- ✔️ Auto Lot Calculation ตาม Risk %
- ✔️ Fixed Lot Size option
- ✔️ Min/Max lot protection

### ✅ **3. Stop Loss & Take Profit:**
- ✔️ Dynamic SL/TP ตาม Swing Points
- ✔️ Fixed SL/TP option
- ✔️ Risk:Reward ratio setting
- ✔️ SL validation (min/max)

### ✅ **4. Trailing Stop:**
- ✔️ Standard trailing stop
- ✔️ Follow trend line trailing
- ✔️ Adjustable start/step

### ✅ **5. Time Filter:**
- ✔️ กรองเวลาเทรด (ชั่วโมง)
- ✔️ กรองวันในสัปดาห์

### ✅ **6. Risk Management:**
- ✔️ Max positions limit
- ✔️ One trade per signal
- ✔️ Minimum time between trades

### ✅ **7. Dashboard:**
- ✔️ Real-time status display
- ✔️ Statistics (Win/Loss, Win Rate)
- ✔️ Current profit/loss
- ✔️ Account info
- ✔️ Swing points

### ✅ **8. Statistics:**
- ✔️ บันทึกสถิติอัตโนมัติ
- ✔️ Load stats เมื่อเปิดใหม่

## 📝 **วิธีใช้งาน:**

### 1. **การติดตั้ง:**
```
1. บันทึกเป็น "Geno_Smart_EA_V23.mq5"
2. วางใน MQL5/Experts/
3. Compile (F7)
4. Drag & Drop ลงบน chart
5. กด OK
```

### 2. **การตั้งค่าพื้นฐาน:**
```
- Enable Auto Trading: true
- Min Signal Confirmation: 2
- Risk Percent: 1.0%
- Risk:Reward: 2.0
- Use Dynamic SL/TP: true
- Use Trailing Stop: true
```

### 3. **การ Backtest:**
```
1. เปิด Strategy Tester (Ctrl+R)
2. เลือก EA: Geno_Smart_EA_V23
3. เลือก Symbol & Timeframe
4. เลือกช่วงเวลา
5. กด Start
```

1. **ทดสอบก่อน** - ใช้ Demo account ก่อนเสมอ

3. **Monitoring** - ติดตามผลและปรับแต่ง
4. **Optimization** - Backtest และ optimize parameters
