# 🔧 แก้ไข EA ให้เปิดไม้ง่ายขึ้น + Debug System

ผมจะแก้ไข EA ให้:
1. **เปิดไม้ง่ายขึ้น** (ลดเงื่อนไข)
2. **เพิ่ม Debug System** (ดูว่าเกิดอะไรขึ้น)
3. **แสดงสัญญาณบนหน้าจอ** (รู้ว่าระบบไหนให้สัญญาณอะไร)

---

## 📝 **EA Version ใหม่ที่แก้ไขแล้ว:**

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                                  Geno_Smart_EA_V23_Debug.mq5     |
//|                      Auto Trading with Debug System              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Geno Smart EA V2.3 - Debug Mode"
#property version   "2.31"
#property strict

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - EA Settings                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ EA SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpAutoTrading = true;              // Enable Auto Trading
input int     InpMagicNumber = 123456;            // Magic Number
input string  InpTradeComment = "Geno_V23";       // Trade Comment
input bool    InpOnlyOneTradePerSignal = false;   // Only One Trade Per Signal ⚠️ เปลี่ยนเป็น false
input int     InpMaxPositions = 3;                // Max Open Positions ⚠️ เพิ่มเป็น 3
input bool    InpCloseOppositeSignal = true;      // Close on Opposite Signal
input int     InpSlippage = 50;                   // Slippage (Points)

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Signal Settings                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ SIGNAL SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpUseSmartTrail = true;            // Use Smart Trail Signals
input bool    InpUseFollowLine1 = true;           // Use Follow Line 1 Signals
input bool    InpUseFollowLine2 = true;           // Use Follow Line 2 Signals
input int     InpMinSignalConfirmation = 1;       // Min Signal Confirmation ⚠️ เปลี่ยนเป็น 1
input bool    InpAggressiveMode = true;           // Aggressive Mode (Easy Entry) 🆕

input group "════════════ TRAIL CALCULATION ════════════"
input bool    InpModifiedTrail = true;            // Modified Trail
input int     InpATRPeriod = 200;                 // ATR Period
input double  InpATRFactor = 4.2;                 // ATR Factor (2-26)
input int     InpSmoothing = 4;                   // Smoothing

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Money Management                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ MONEY MANAGEMENT ════════════"
input bool    InpUseAutoLotSize = false;          // Use Auto Lot Calculation ⚠️ ปิดไว้ก่อน
input double  InpFixedLotSize = 0.01;             // Fixed Lot Size
input double  InpRiskPercent = 1.0;               // Risk % per Trade
input double  InpMaxLotSize = 10.0;               // Maximum Lot Size
input double  InpMinLotSize = 0.01;               // Minimum Lot Size

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Stop Loss & Take Profit                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ STOP LOSS & TAKE PROFIT ════════════"
input bool    InpUseDynamicSL = true;             // Use Dynamic SL (Swing Points)
input int     InpFixedSLPoints = 500;             // Fixed SL in Points
input bool    InpUseDynamicTP = true;             // Use Dynamic TP (Risk:Reward)
input double  InpRiskRewardRatio = 2.0;           // Risk:Reward Ratio
input int     InpFixedTPPoints = 1000;            // Fixed TP in Points
input int     InpMinSLPoints = 50;                // Minimum SL in Points ⚠️ ลดลง
input int     InpMaxSLPoints = 5000;              // Maximum SL in Points ⚠️ เพิ่มขึ้น
input int     InpSLBuffer = 50;                   // SL Buffer in Points ⚠️ ลดลง

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Trailing Stop                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ TRAILING STOP ════════════"
input bool    InpUseTrailingStop = true;          // Enable Trailing Stop
input int     InpTrailingStart = 200;             // Trailing Start (Points)
input int     InpTrailingStep = 100;              // Trailing Step (Points)
input bool    InpTrailingFollowLine = true;       // Trail Follow Line

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Time Filter                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ TIME FILTER ════════════"
input bool    InpUseTimeFilter = false;           // Enable Time Filter ⚠️ ปิดไว้
input int     InpStartHour = 0;                   // Start Hour (0-23)
input int     InpEndHour = 23;                    // End Hour (0-23)
input bool    InpTradeMonday = true;              // Trade on Monday
input bool    InpTradeTuesday = true;             // Trade on Tuesday
input bool    InpTradeWednesday = true;           // Trade on Wednesday
input bool    InpTradeThursday = true;            // Trade on Thursday
input bool    InpTradeFriday = true;              // Trade on Friday

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Follow Line Settings                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ FOLLOW LINE 1 ════════════"
input int     InpBBPeriod1 = 21;                  // BB Period
input double  InpBBDeviation1 = 1.0;              // BB Deviations
input bool    InpUseATRFilter1 = true;            // Use ATR Filter
input int     InpATRPeriod1 = 5;                  // ATR Period

input group "════════════ FOLLOW LINE 2 (MA) ════════════"
input bool    InpUseCurrentTF = true;             // Use Current Timeframe
input ENUM_TIMEFRAMES InpMATF = PERIOD_H1;        // MA Timeframe
input int     InpMAPeriod = 21;                   // MA Period
input ENUM_MA_METHOD InpMAMethod = MODE_EMA;      // MA Method
input double  InpBBDeviations = 1.0;              // BB Deviations
input int     InpATRPeriod2 = 5;                  // ATR Period

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - Dashboard & Debug                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ DASHBOARD & DEBUG ════════════"
input bool    InpShowDashboard = true;            // Show Dashboard
input bool    InpShowDebugInfo = true;            // Show Debug Info 🆕
input bool    InpPrintSignals = true;             // Print Signals to Log 🆕
input ENUM_BASE_CORNER InpDashCorner = CORNER_LEFT_UPPER; // Dashboard Corner
input color   InpDashTitleCol = clrDodgerBlue;    // Title Color
input color   InpDashTextCol = clrWhite;          // Text Color

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Global Variables                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
// Indicator Handles
int atrHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;
int atrHandle1 = INVALID_HANDLE;
int atrHandle2 = INVALID_HANDLE;
int maHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;

// Signal Variables
bool g_buySignal = false;
bool g_sellSignal = false;
bool g_lastTradeWasBuy = false;
bool g_lastTradeWasSell = false;
datetime g_lastBarTime = 0;

// Trade Management
double g_lastSwingHigh = 0;
double g_lastSwingLow = 0;
datetime g_lastTradeTime = 0;

// Trail Calculation Buffers
double g_trailBuffer[];
double g_trendUpBuffer[];
double g_trendDownBuffer[];
double g_trendLine1Buffer[];
double g_trendLineBuffer[];

// Statistics
int g_totalTrades = 0;
int g_winTrades = 0;
int g_loseTrades = 0;
double g_totalProfit = 0;
double g_totalLoss = 0;

// 🆕 Debug Variables
int g_smartTrailSignal = 0;     // 1=BUY, -1=SELL, 0=WAIT
int g_followLine1Signal = 0;
int g_followLine2Signal = 0;
int g_totalBuySignals = 0;
int g_totalSellSignals = 0;
string g_debugMessage = "";
bool g_buffersReady = false;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert initialization function                                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
    // Initialize indicator handles
    atrHandle = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod);
    atrHandle1 = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod1);
    atrHandle2 = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod2);
    
    ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf = InpUseCurrentTF ? PERIOD_CURRENT : InpMATF;
    maHandle = iMA(_Symbol, tf, InpMAPeriod, 0, InpMAMethod, PRICE_CLOSE);
    
    if(atrHandle == INVALID_HANDLE || atrHandle1 == INVALID_HANDLE || 
       atrHandle2 == INVALID_HANDLE || maHandle == INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        Print("❌ Error creating indicator handles");
        return(INIT_FAILED);
    }
    
    // Initialize buffers
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trailBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendUpBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendDownBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendLine1Buffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendLineBuffer, true);
    
    ArrayResize(g_trailBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendUpBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendDownBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendLine1Buffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendLineBuffer, 500);
    
    ArrayInitialize(g_trailBuffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendUpBuffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendDownBuffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendLine1Buffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendLineBuffer, 0);
    
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("🚀 Geno Smart EA V2.3 - DEBUG MODE");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Auto Trading: ", InpAutoTrading ? "✅ ENABLED" : "⛔ DISABLED");
    Print("Aggressive Mode: ", InpAggressiveMode ? "✅ ON (Easy Entry)" : "⚠️ OFF");
    Print("Min Signal Confirmation: ", InpMinSignalConfirmation);
    Print("Magic Number: ", InpMagicNumber);
    Print("Symbol: ", _Symbol);
    Print("Timeframe: ", EnumToString(PERIOD_CURRENT));
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    
    return(INIT_SUCCEEDED);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert deinitialization function                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
    if(atrHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(atrHandle);
    if(atrHandle1 != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(atrHandle1);
    if(atrHandle2 != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(atrHandle2);
    if(maHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(maHandle);
    
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DEBUG_");
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "SIGNAL_");
    
    Comment("");
    Print("EA deinitialized - Reason: ", reason);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert tick function                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
    // Check if new bar
    if(!IsNewBar())
    {
        // Update dashboard on every tick
        if(InpShowDashboard)
            UpdateDashboard();
        return;
    }
    
    // Check if auto trading is enabled
    if(!InpAutoTrading)
    {
        if(InpShowDashboard)
            UpdateDashboard();
        return;
    }
    
    // Check time filter
    if(InpUseTimeFilter && !CheckTimeFilter())
    {
        if(InpPrintSignals)
            Print("⏰ Time filter blocked trading");
        return;
    }
    
    // Calculate indicators
    CalculateIndicators();
    
    // Check if buffers are ready
    if(!g_buffersReady)
    {
        if(InpPrintSignals)
            Print("⏳ Waiting for buffers to initialize...");
        return;
    }
    
    // Check for signals
    CheckForSignals();
    
    // Manage open positions
    ManagePositions();
    
    // Update dashboard
    if(InpShowDashboard)
        UpdateDashboard();
    
    // Draw signals on chart
    if(InpShowDebugInfo)
        DrawSignalsOnChart();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if new bar                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsNewBar()
{
    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    
    if(g_lastBarTime != currentBarTime)
    {
        g_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
        return true;
    }
    
    return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check time filter                                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CheckTimeFilter()
{
    MqlDateTime dt;
    TimeToStruct(TimeCurrent(), dt);
    
    if(dt.hour < InpStartHour || dt.hour >= InpEndHour)
        return false;
    
    switch(dt.day_of_week)
    {
        case 1: if(!InpTradeMonday) return false; break;
        case 2: if(!InpTradeTuesday) return false; break;
        case 3: if(!InpTradeWednesday) return false; break;
        case 4: if(!InpTradeThursday) return false; break;
        case 5: if(!InpTradeFriday) return false; break;
        default: return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Indicators                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicators()
{
    double high[], low[], close[], open[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(high, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(low, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(close, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(open, true);
    
    int bars = 100;
    CopyHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, high);
    CopyLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, low);
    CopyClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, close);
    CopyOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, open);
    
    double atr[], atr1[], atr2[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr1, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr2, true);
    
    CopyBuffer(atrHandle, 0, 0, bars, atr);
    CopyBuffer(atrHandle1, 0, 0, bars, atr1);
    CopyBuffer(atrHandle2, 0, 0, bars, atr2);
    
    double ma[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(ma, true);
    CopyBuffer(maHandle, 0, 0, bars, ma);
    
    // Calculate Smart Trail
    if(InpUseSmartTrail)
        CalculateSmartTrail(high, low, close, atr);
    
    // Calculate Follow Line 1
    if(InpUseFollowLine1)
        CalculateFollowLine1(high, low, close, atr1);
    
    // Calculate Follow Line 2
    if(InpUseFollowLine2)
        CalculateFollowLine2(high, low, close, ma, atr2);
    
    // Check if buffers are ready
    CheckBuffersReady();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if Buffers are Ready                                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckBuffersReady()
{
    bool ready = true;
    
    if(InpUseSmartTrail)
    {
        if(g_trailBuffer[0] == 0 || g_trailBuffer[1] == 0 || g_trailBuffer[2] == 0)
            ready = false;
    }
    
    if(InpUseFollowLine1)
    {
        if(g_trendLine1Buffer[0] == 0 || g_trendLine1Buffer[1] == 0 || g_trendLine1Buffer[2] == 0)
            ready = false;
    }
    
    if(InpUseFollowLine2)
    {
        if(g_trendLineBuffer[0] == 0 || g_trendLineBuffer[1] == 0 || g_trendLineBuffer[2] == 0)
            ready = false;
    }
    
    if(ready && !g_buffersReady)
    {
        g_buffersReady = true;
        Print("✅ Buffers are ready! EA is now active.");
    }
    
    g_buffersReady = ready;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Smart Trail                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateSmartTrail(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                         const double &close[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    double trueRange = CalculateTrueRange(shift, high, low, close);
    
    static double wild_prev = 0;
    double wild = (wild_prev == 0) ? trueRange : wild_prev + (trueRange - wild_prev) / InpATRPeriod;
    wild_prev = wild;
    
    double loss = InpATRFactor * wild;
    
    double Up68 = close[shift] - loss;
    double Dn68 = close[shift] + loss;
    
    if(shift == 0 && g_trendUpBuffer[1] != 0)
    {
        if(close[1] > g_trendUpBuffer[1])
            g_trendUpBuffer[0] = MathMax(Up68, g_trendUpBuffer[1]);
        else
            g_trendUpBuffer[0] = Up68;
        
        if(close[1] < g_trendDownBuffer[1])
            g_trendDownBuffer[0] = MathMin(Dn68, g_trendDownBuffer[1]);
        else
            g_trendDownBuffer[0] = Dn68;
    }
    else
    {
        g_trendUpBuffer[0] = Up68;
        g_trendDownBuffer[0] = Dn68;
    }
    
    int trend = 0;
    if(g_trailBuffer[1] != 0)
    {
        if(close[0] > g_trendDownBuffer[1])
            trend = 1;
        else if(close[0] < g_trendUpBuffer[1])
            trend = -1;
        else
            trend = (g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendUpBuffer[1]) ? 1 : -1;
    }
    else
    {
        trend = (close[0] > (Up68 + Dn68) / 2) ? 1 : -1;
    }
    
    g_trailBuffer[0] = (trend == 1) ? g_trendUpBuffer[0] : g_trendDownBuffer[0];
    
    if(trend == 1)
        g_lastSwingLow = g_trailBuffer[0];
    else
        g_lastSwingHigh = g_trailBuffer[0];
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate True Range                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateTrueRange(int shift, const double &high[], 
                          const double &low[], const double &close[])
{
    if(shift >= ArraySize(high) - 1)
        return high[shift] - low[shift];
    
    double tr1 = high[shift] - low[shift];
    double tr2 = MathAbs(high[shift] - close[shift+1]);
    double tr3 = MathAbs(low[shift] - close[shift+1]);
    
    return MathMax(tr1, MathMax(tr2, tr3));
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 1                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine1(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                          const double &close[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    double sma = 0;
    double sum = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < InpBBPeriod1; i++)
    {
        sma += close[shift + i];
    }
    sma /= InpBBPeriod1;
    
    for(int i = 0; i < InpBBPeriod1; i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[shift + i] - sma, 2);
    }
    double stdev = MathSqrt(sum / InpBBPeriod1);
    
    double BBUpper = sma + stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    double BBLower = sma - stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    
    static double TrendLine1_prev = 0;
    
    double TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    int BBSignal = 0;
    
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper)
        BBSignal = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower)
        BBSignal = -1;
    
    if(BBSignal == 1)
    {
        TrendLine1 = InpUseATRFilter1 ? (low[shift] - atr[shift]) : low[shift];
        if(TrendLine1_prev != 0 && TrendLine1 < TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal == -1)
    {
        TrendLine1 = InpUseATRFilter1 ? (high[shift] + atr[shift]) : high[shift];
        if(TrendLine1_prev != 0 && TrendLine1 > TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    
    if(TrendLine1 == 0)
        TrendLine1 = close[shift];
    
    g_trendLine1Buffer[0] = TrendLine1;
    TrendLine1_prev = TrendLine1;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 2                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine2(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                          const double &close[], const double &ma[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    double sum = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < InpMAPeriod; i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[shift + i] - ma[shift], 2);
    }
    double stdev = MathSqrt(sum / InpMAPeriod);
    
    double BBUpper = ma[shift] + stdev * InpBBDeviations;
    double BBLower = ma[shift] - stdev * InpBBDeviations;
    
    static double TrendLine_prev = 0;
    static int iTrend_prev = 0;
    
    double TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    int BBSignal = 0;
    
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper)
        BBSignal = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower)
        BBSignal = -1;
    
    double v = atr[shift];
    
    if(BBSignal == 1)
    {
        TrendLine = low[shift] - v;
        if(TrendLine_prev != 0 && TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal == -1)
    {
        TrendLine = high[shift] + v;
        if(TrendLine_prev != 0 && TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    
    if(TrendLine == 0)
        TrendLine = close[shift];
    
    int iTrend = iTrend_prev;
    if(TrendLine_prev != 0)
    {
        if(TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
            iTrend = 1;
        else if(TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
            iTrend = -1;
    }
    
    g_trendLineBuffer[0] = TrendLine;
    
    if(iTrend_prev == -1 && iTrend == 1)
        g_lastSwingLow = TrendLine;
    
    if(iTrend_prev == 1 && iTrend == -1)
        g_lastSwingHigh = TrendLine;
    
    TrendLine_prev = TrendLine;
    iTrend_prev = iTrend;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check For Trading Signals                                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForSignals()
{
    g_buySignal = false;
    g_sellSignal = false;
    
    int buyCount = 0;
    int sellCount = 0;
    
    g_smartTrailSignal = 0;
    g_followLine1Signal = 0;
    g_followLine2Signal = 0;
    
    g_debugMessage = "🔍 SIGNAL ANALYSIS:\n";
    
    // === Check Smart Trail Signal ===
    if(InpUseSmartTrail && g_trailBuffer[1] != 0 && g_trailBuffer[2] != 0)
    {
        bool trailCrossedUp = (g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendUpBuffer[1] && g_trailBuffer[2] == g_trendDownBuffer[2]);
        bool trailCrossedDown = (g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendDownBuffer[1] && g_trailBuffer[2] == g_trendUpBuffer[2]);
        
        // 🆕 Aggressive Mode: ไม่ต้องรอ cross ถ้าอยู่ใน trend
        if(InpAggressiveMode)
        {
            if(g_trailBuffer[0] == g_trendUpBuffer[0])
            {
                g_smartTrailSignal = 1;
                buyCount++;
                g_debugMessage += "📈 Smart Trail: BUY (Uptrend)\n";
            }
            else if(g_trailBuffer[0] == g_trendDownBuffer[0])
            {
                g_smartTrailSignal = -1;
                sellCount++;
                g_debugMessage += "📉 Smart Trail: SELL (Downtrend)\n";
            }
        }
        else
        {
            if(trailCrossedUp)
            {
                g_smartTrailSignal = 1;
                buyCount++;
                g_debugMessage += "📈 Smart Trail: BUY (Crossed Up)\n";
            }
            else if(trailCrossedDown)
            {
                g_smartTrailSignal = -1;
                sellCount++;
                g_debugMessage += "📉 Smart Trail: SELL (Crossed Down)\n";
            }
            else
            {
                g_debugMessage += "⏸️ Smart Trail: WAIT\n";
            }
        }
    }
    
    // === Check Follow Line 1 Signal ===
    if(InpUseFollowLine1 && g_trendLine1Buffer[1] != 0 && g_trendLine1Buffer[2] != 0)
    {
        if(g_trendLine1Buffer[1] > g_trendLine1Buffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine1Signal = 1;
            buyCount++;
            g_debugMessage += "📈 Follow Line 1: BUY\n";
        }
        else if(g_trendLine1Buffer[1] < g_trendLine1Buffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine1Signal = -1;
            sellCount++;
            g_debugMessage += "📉 Follow Line 1: SELL\n";
        }
        else
        {
            g_debugMessage += "⏸️ Follow Line 1: WAIT\n";
        }
    }
    
    // === Check Follow Line 2 Signal ===
    if(InpUseFollowLine2 && g_trendLineBuffer[1] != 0 && g_trendLineBuffer[2] != 0)
    {
        if(g_trendLineBuffer[1] > g_trendLineBuffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine2Signal = 1;
            buyCount++;
            g_debugMessage += "📈 Follow Line 2: BUY\n";
        }
        else if(g_trendLineBuffer[1] < g_trendLineBuffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine2Signal = -1;
            sellCount++;
            g_debugMessage += "📉 Follow Line 2: SELL\n";
        }
        else
        {
            g_debugMessage += "⏸️ Follow Line 2: WAIT\n";
        }
    }
    
    g_totalBuySignals = buyCount;
    g_totalSellSignals = sellCount;
    
    // Determine final signal
    g_debugMessage += "\n📊 RESULT:\n";
    g_debugMessage += StringFormat("  BUY Signals: %d / %d\n", buyCount, InpMinSignalConfirmation);
    g_debugMessage += StringFormat("  SELL Signals: %d / %d\n", sellCount, InpMinSignalConfirmation);
    
    if(buyCount >= InpMinSignalConfirmation)
    {
        g_buySignal = true;
        g_debugMessage += "\n✅ SIGNAL: OPEN BUY!";
    }
    
    if(sellCount >= InpMinSignalConfirmation)
    {
        g_sellSignal = true;
        g_debugMessage += "\n✅ SIGNAL: OPEN SELL!";
    }
    
    if(!g_buySignal && !g_sellSignal)
    {
        g_debugMessage += "\n⏸️ SIGNAL: WAIT (Not enough confirmation)";
    }
    
    // Print to log
    if(InpPrintSignals && (g_buySignal || g_sellSignal))
    {
        Print(g_debugMessage);
    }
    
    // Execute trades
    if(g_buySignal)
    {
        if(InpCloseOppositeSignal)
            ClosePositions(POSITION_TYPE_SELL);
        
        if(CanOpenNewTrade(ORDER_TYPE_BUY))
            OpenBuyPosition();
    }
    
    if(g_sellSignal)
    {
        if(InpCloseOppositeSignal)
            ClosePositions(POSITION_TYPE_BUY);
        
        if(CanOpenNewTrade(ORDER_TYPE_SELL))
            OpenSellPosition();
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Can Open New Trade                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CanOpenNewTrade(ENUM_ORDER_TYPE orderType)
{
    if(CountOpenPositions() >= InpMaxPositions)
    {
        if(InpPrintSignals)
            Print("⚠️ Cannot open trade: Max positions reached (", InpMaxPositions, ")");
        return false;
    }
    
    if(InpOnlyOneTradePerSignal)
    {
        if(orderType == ORDER_TYPE_BUY && g_lastTradeWasBuy)
        {
            if(InpPrintSignals)
                Print("⚠️ Cannot open BUY: Already traded this signal");
            return false;
        }
        if(orderType == ORDER_TYPE_SELL && g_lastTradeWasSell)
        {
            if(InpPrintSignals)
                Print("⚠️ Cannot open SELL: Already traded this signal");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    if(TimeCurrent() - g_lastTradeTime < 60)
    {
        if(InpPrintSignals)
            Print("⚠️ Cannot open trade: Too soon (< 60 seconds)");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Count Open Positions                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CountOpenPositions()
{
    int count = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                count++;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return count;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Buy Position                                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenBuyPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
    double sl = 0, tp = 0;
    
    // Calculate SL
    if(InpUseDynamicSL && g_lastSwingLow > 0)
    {
        sl = g_lastSwingLow - (InpSLBuffer * _Point);
        
        double slDistance = price - sl;
        if(slDistance < InpMinSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price - (InpMinSLPoints * _Point);
        if(slDistance > InpMaxSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price - (InpMaxSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    else
    {
        sl = price - (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Calculate TP
    if(InpUseDynamicTP)
    {
        double slDistance = price - sl;
        tp = price + (slDistance * InpRiskRewardRatio);
    }
    else
    {
        tp = price + (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    double lotSize = InpUseAutoLotSize ? 
                     CalculateLotSize(price, sl) : InpFixedLotSize;
    
    lotSize = NormalizeLotSize(lotSize);
    
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = lotSize;
    request.type = ORDER_TYPE_BUY;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.deviation = InpSlippage;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = InpTradeComment;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    if(OrderSend(request, result))
    {
        if(result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE || result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_PLACED)
        {
            g_lastTradeWasBuy = true;
            g_lastTradeWasSell = false;
            g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
            g_totalTrades++;
            
            Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
            Print("✅ BUY POSITION OPENED SUCCESSFULLY!");
            Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
            Print("Ticket: ", result.order);
            Print("Price: ", result.price);
            Print("SL: ", sl, " (", DoubleToString((price - sl) / _Point, 0), " points)");
            Print("TP: ", tp, " (", DoubleToString((tp - price) / _Point, 0), " points)");
            Print("Lots: ", lotSize);
            Print("Risk:Reward: 1:", DoubleToString(InpRiskRewardRatio, 1));
            Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
        }
        else
        {
            Print("❌ Error opening BUY: ", result.retcode, " - ", GetErrorDescription(result.retcode));
        }
    }
    else
    {
        Print("❌ OrderSend failed: ", GetLastError());
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Sell Position                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenSellPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    double sl = 0, tp = 0;
    
    // Calculate SL
    if(InpUseDynamicSL && g_lastSwingHigh > 0)
    {
        sl = g_lastSwingHigh + (InpSLBuffer * _Point);
        
        double slDistance = sl - price;
        if(slDistance < InpMinSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price + (InpMinSLPoints * _Point);
        if(slDistance > InpMaxSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price + (InpMaxSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    else
    {
        sl = price + (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Calculate TP
    if(InpUseDynamicTP)
    {
        double slDistance = sl - price;
        tp = price - (slDistance * InpRiskRewardRatio);
    }
    else
    {
        tp = price - (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    double lotSize = InpUseAutoLotSize ? 
                     CalculateLotSize(price, sl) : InpFixedLotSize;
    
    lotSize = NormalizeLotSize(lotSize);
    
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = lotSize;
    request.type = ORDER_TYPE_SELL;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.deviation = InpSlippage;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = InpTradeComment;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    if(OrderSend(request, result))
    {
        if(result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE || result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_PLACED)
        {
            g_lastTradeWasSell = true;
            g_lastTradeWasBuy = false;
            g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
            g_totalTrades++;
            
            Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
            Print("✅ SELL POSITION OPENED SUCCESSFULLY!");
            Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
            Print("Ticket: ", result.order);
            Print("Price: ", result.price);
            Print("SL: ", sl, " (", DoubleToString((sl - price) / _Point, 0), " points)");
            Print("TP: ", tp, " (", DoubleToString((price - tp) / _Point, 0), " points)");
            Print("Lots: ", lotSize);
            Print("Risk:Reward: 1:", DoubleToString(InpRiskRewardRatio, 1));
            Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
        }
        else
        {
            Print("❌ Error opening SELL: ", result.retcode, " - ", GetErrorDescription(result.retcode));
        }
    }
    else
    {
        Print("❌ OrderSend failed: ", GetLastError());
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Lot Size                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateLotSize(double entryPrice, double stopLoss)
{
    double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    double riskAmount = balance * (InpRiskPercent / 100.0);
    double slDistance = MathAbs(entryPrice - stopLoss);
    
    if(slDistance == 0)
        return InpMinLotSize;
    
    double tickValue = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
    double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
    
    if(tickSize == 0 || tickValue == 0)
        return InpMinLotSize;
    
    double lotSize = (riskAmount * tickSize) / (slDistance * tickValue);
    
    return lotSize;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Normalize Lot Size                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double NormalizeLotSize(double lotSize)
{
    double minLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
    double maxLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
    double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
    
    lotSize = MathMax(lotSize, InpMinLotSize);
    lotSize = MathMin(lotSize, InpMaxLotSize);
    lotSize = MathMax(lotSize, minLot);
    lotSize = MathMin(lotSize, maxLot);
    
    if(lotStep > 0)
        lotSize = MathRound(lotSize / lotStep) * lotStep;
    
    return NormalizeDouble(lotSize, 2);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Manage Open Positions                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManagePositions()
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0)
            continue;
        
        if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != _Symbol || 
           PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        if(InpUseTrailingStop)
            ApplyTrailingStop(ticket);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Apply Trailing Stop                                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ApplyTrailingStop(ulong ticket)
{
    if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket))
        return;
    
    double currentSL = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
    double currentTP = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
    double openPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
    ENUM_POSITION_TYPE posType = (ENUM_POSITION_TYPE)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
    
    double newSL = currentSL;
    
    if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
    {
        double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
        double profit = currentPrice - openPrice;
        
        if(profit >= InpTrailingStart * _Point)
        {
            if(InpTrailingFollowLine && g_trailBuffer[0] > 0)
            {
                newSL = g_trailBuffer[0];
            }
            else
            {
                newSL = currentPrice - (InpTrailingStep * _Point);
            }
            
            newSL = NormalizeDouble(newSL, _Digits);
            
            if(newSL > currentSL && newSL < currentPrice)
            {
                ModifyPosition(ticket, newSL, currentTP);
            }
        }
    }
    else if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_SELL)
    {
        double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
        double profit = openPrice - currentPrice;
        
        if(profit >= InpTrailingStart * _Point)
        {
            if(InpTrailingFollowLine && g_trailBuffer[0] > 0)
            {
                newSL = g_trailBuffer[0];
            }
            else
            {
                newSL = currentPrice + (InpTrailingStep * _Point);
            }
            
            newSL = NormalizeDouble(newSL, _Digits);
            
            if((newSL < currentSL || currentSL == 0) && newSL > currentPrice)
            {
                ModifyPosition(ticket, newSL, currentTP);
            }
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Modify Position                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool ModifyPosition(ulong ticket, double sl, double tp)
{
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_SLTP;
    request.position = ticket;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    
    if(OrderSend(request, result))
    {
        if(result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE)
        {
            Print("✓ Position modified: Ticket=", ticket, " SL=", sl, " TP=", tp);
            return true;
        }
    }
    
    return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Close Positions                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ClosePositions(ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type)
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0)
            continue;
        
        if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != _Symbol || 
           PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        ENUM_POSITION_TYPE posType = (ENUM_POSITION_TYPE)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
        
        if(posType == type)
        {
            MqlTradeRequest request = {};
            MqlTradeResult result = {};
            
            request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
            request.position = ticket;
            request.symbol = _Symbol;
            request.volume = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
            request.type = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? ORDER_TYPE_SELL : ORDER_TYPE_BUY;
            request.price = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? 
                           SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID) : 
                           SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
            request.deviation = InpSlippage;
            request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
            request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
            
            if(OrderSend(request, result))
            {
                Print("✓ Position closed by opposite signal: Ticket=", ticket);
            }
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Dashboard                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateDashboard()
{
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    int x = 20;
    int y = 30;
    int lineHeight = 18;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE", x, y, "═══ GENO SMART EA V2.3 DEBUG ═══", InpDashTitleCol, 10);
    y += lineHeight + 5;
    
    string status = InpAutoTrading ? "ENABLED" : "DISABLED";
    color statusColor = InpAutoTrading ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATUS", x, y, "Status: " + status, statusColor, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    string mode = InpAggressiveMode ? "AGGRESSIVE (Easy)" : "NORMAL";
    CreateLabel("DASH_MODE", x, y, "Mode: " + mode, clrYellow, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Buffers Ready
    string buffersStatus = g_buffersReady ? "✅ READY" : "⏳ LOADING...";
    color buffersColor = g_buffersReady ? clrLime : clrOrange;
    CreateLabel("DASH_BUFFERS", x, y, "Buffers: " + buffersStatus, buffersColor, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_SIGNALS_TITLE", x, y, "─── SIGNALS ───", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Smart Trail Signal
    string stSignal = g_smartTrailSignal == 1 ? "BUY" : g_smartTrailSignal == -1 ? "SELL" : "WAIT";
    color stColor = g_smartTrailSignal == 1 ? clrLime : g_smartTrailSignal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_ST_SIGNAL", x, y, "Smart Trail: " + stSignal, stColor, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Follow Line 1 Signal
    string fl1Signal = g_followLine1Signal == 1 ? "BUY" : g_followLine1Signal == -1 ? "SELL" : "WAIT";
    color fl1Color = g_followLine1Signal == 1 ? clrLime : g_followLine1Signal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FL1_SIGNAL", x, y, "Follow Line 1: " + fl1Signal, fl1Color, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Follow Line 2 Signal
    string fl2Signal = g_followLine2Signal == 1 ? "BUY" : g_followLine2Signal == -1 ? "SELL" : "WAIT";
    color fl2Color = g_followLine2Signal == 1 ? clrLime : g_followLine2Signal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FL2_SIGNAL", x, y, "Follow Line 2: " + fl2Signal, fl2Color, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_RESULT_TITLE", x, y, "─── RESULT ───", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_BUY_COUNT", x, y, StringFormat("BUY: %d/%d", g_totalBuySignals, InpMinSignalConfirmation), 
                g_totalBuySignals >= InpMinSignalConfirmation ? clrLime : InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_SELL_COUNT", x, y, StringFormat("SELL: %d/%d", g_totalSellSignals, InpMinSignalConfirmation), 
                g_totalSellSignals >= InpMinSignalConfirmation ? clrRed : InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    string finalSignal = g_buySignal ? "🚀 OPEN BUY!" : g_sellSignal ? "🚀 OPEN SELL!" : "⏸️ WAIT";
    color finalColor = g_buySignal ? clrLime : g_sellSignal ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FINAL_SIGNAL", x, y, finalSignal, finalColor, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    int openPos = CountOpenPositions();
    CreateLabel("DASH_POSITIONS", x, y, "Open: " + IntegerToString(openPos), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    double currentProfit = CalculateCurrentProfit();
    color profitColor = currentProfit >= 0 ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_PROFIT", x, y, "P/L: " + DoubleToString(currentProfit, 2), profitColor, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    y += 5;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATS_TITLE", x, y, "─── Statistics ───", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_TOTAL", x, y, "Total: " + IntegerToString(g_totalTrades), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    CreateLabel("DASH_BALANCE", x, y, "Balance: " + DoubleToString(balance, 2), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += lineHeight;
    
    // Swing Points
    CreateLabel("DASH_SWING_HIGH", x, y, "Swing High: " + DoubleToString(g_lastSwingHigh, _Digits), clrOrange, 7);
    y += lineHeight - 2;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_SWING_LOW", x, y, "Swing Low: " + DoubleToString(g_lastSwingLow, _Digits), clrAqua, 7);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Draw Signals On Chart                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void DrawSignalsOnChart()
{
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "SIGNAL_");
    
    double price = (iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0) + iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0)) / 2;
    datetime time = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    
    if(g_buySignal)
    {
        ObjectCreate(0, "SIGNAL_BUY", OBJ_ARROW, 0, time, price);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SIGNAL_BUY", OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 233);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SIGNAL_BUY", OBJPROP_COLOR, clrLime);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SIGNAL_BUY", OBJPROP_WIDTH, 3);
    }
    
    if(g_sellSignal)
    {
        ObjectCreate(0, "SIGNAL_SELL", OBJ_ARROW, 0, time, price);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SIGNAL_SELL", OBJPROP_ARROWCODE, 234);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SIGNAL_SELL", OBJPROP_COLOR, clrRed);
        ObjectSetInteger(0, "SIGNAL_SELL", OBJPROP_WIDTH, 3);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Create Label                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateLabel(string name, int x, int y, string text, color clr, int size = 8)
{
    ObjectCreate(0, name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_CORNER, InpDashCorner);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, x);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, y);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_TEXT, text);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clr);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, size);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Arial Bold");
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Current Profit                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateCurrentProfit()
{
    double profit = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                profit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
                profit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP);
            }
        }
    }
    
    return profit;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Get Error Description                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
string GetErrorDescription(uint retcode)
{
    switch(retcode)
    {
        case TRADE_RETCODE_DONE: return "Request completed";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_PLACED: return "Order placed";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_DONE_PARTIAL: return "Partial fill";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_ERROR: return "Request error";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_TIMEOUT: return "Request timeout";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_INVALID: return "Invalid request";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_INVALID_VOLUME: return "Invalid volume";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_INVALID_PRICE: return "Invalid price";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_INVALID_STOPS: return "Invalid stops";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_TRADE_DISABLED: return "Trade disabled";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_MARKET_CLOSED: return "Market closed";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_NO_MONEY: return "Not enough money";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_PRICE_CHANGED: return "Price changed";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_PRICE_OFF: return "No prices";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_INVALID_EXPIRATION: return "Invalid expiration";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_ORDER_CHANGED: return "Order changed";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS: return "Too many requests";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_NO_CHANGES: return "No changes";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_SERVER_DISABLES_AT: return "Autotrading disabled by server";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_CLIENT_DISABLES_AT: return "Autotrading disabled by client";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_LOCKED: return "Request locked";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_FROZEN: return "Order frozen";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_INVALID_FILL: return "Invalid fill";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_CONNECTION: return "No connection";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_ONLY_REAL: return "Only real accounts";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_LIMIT_ORDERS: return "Orders limit reached";
        case TRADE_RETCODE_LIMIT_VOLUME: return "Volume limit reached";
        default: return "Unknown error: " + IntegerToString(retcode);
    }
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

---

## 🎯 **สิ่งที่แก้ไข:**

### ✅ **1. ลดความเข้มงวด:**
```cpp
Min Signal Confirmation = 1      // เปลี่ยนจาก 2 เป็น 1 (สัญญาณเดียวก็เปิดได้)
Only One Trade Per Signal = false // ปิด (เปิดได้หลายครั้ง)
Max Positions = 3                 // เพิ่มจาก 1 เป็น 3
Use Time Filter = false           // ปิด (เทรดได้ทุกเวลา)
```

### ✅ **2. เพิ่ม Aggressive Mode:**
```cpp
Aggressive Mode = true  // เปิดไม้ง่ายมาก!

// ในโหมดนี้:
- ไม่ต้องรอ trail cross
- ถ้าอยู่ใน uptrend → ให้สัญญาณ BUY
- ถ้าอยู่ใน downtrend → ให้สัญญาณ SELL
```

### ✅ **3. เพิ่ม Debug System:**
```cpp
Show Debug Info = true   // แสดงสัญญาณบนหน้าจอ
Print Signals = true     // พิมพ์ข้อมูลใน Log

Dashboard จะแสดง:
- สถานะแต่ละระบบ (BUY/SELL/WAIT)
- จำนวนสัญญาณที่ได้รับ
- ว่าจะเปิดไม้หรือไม่
```

### ✅ **4. ลด SL ข้อจำกัด:**
```cpp
Min SL Points = 50     // ลดจาก 100 เป็น 50
Max SL Points = 5000   // เพิ่มจาก 3000 เป็น 5000
SL Buffer = 50         // ลดจาก 100 เป็น 50
```

### ✅ **5. Dashboard ใหม่:**
```
╔═══════════════════════════════════╗
║ GENO SMART EA V2.3 DEBUG          ║
╠═══════════════════════════════════╣
║ Status: ENABLED                   ║
║ Mode: AGGRESSIVE (Easy)           ║
║ Buffers: ✅ READY                 ║
║                                   ║
║ ─── SIGNALS ───                   ║
║ Smart Trail: BUY                  ║
║ Follow Line 1: BUY                ║
║ Follow Line 2: WAIT               ║
║                                   ║
║ ─── RESULT ───                    ║
║ BUY: 2/1  ✅                      ║
║ SELL: 0/1                         ║
║ 🚀 OPEN BUY!                      ║
║                                   ║
║ Open: 0                           ║
║ P/L: 0.00                         ║
╚═══════════════════════════════════╝
```

## 🔍 **การแก้ปัญหาถ้ายังไม่เปิดไม้:**

### **1. ตรวจสอบ Log:**
```
ดูใน Experts tab ว่าเจอข้อความอะไร:

❌ "Waiting for buffers..." → รอ 5-10 นาที
❌ "Time filter blocked" → ปิด Time Filter
❌ "Max positions reached" → เพิ่ม Max Positions
❌ "Too soon" → รอ 60 วินาที
```

### **2. ตั้งค่าเพื่อเปิดง่ายที่สุด:**
```cpp
Min Signal Confirmation = 1
Aggressive Mode = true
Only One Trade Per Signal = false
Max Positions = 10
Use Time Filter = false
```

### **3. ตรวจสอบ Dashboard:**
```
ดูว่า:
- Buffers: ต้องขึ้น "✅ READY"
- Signals: ต้องมีอย่างน้อย 1 BUY หรือ SELL
- Result: ต้องขึ้น "🚀 OPEN BUY/SELL!"
```

----- [user] -----
Buy กับ Sell เป็น 0/2 เป็น wait ตลอดเวลาไม่เปลี่ยนเลย แก้ให้หน่อย

----- [assistant] -----
<details type="reasoning" done="true" duration="3">
<summary>Thought for 3 seconds</summary>
&gt; ปัญหาคือ EA ไม่เคยให้สัญญาณ BUY หรือ SELL เลย แสดงว่า:
&gt; 1. Buffer ไม่ได้ค่า หรือ
&gt; 2. Logic การตรวจสอบสัญญาณมีปัญหา
&gt; 3. ค่าที่คำนวณได้ไม่เปลี่ยนแปลง
&gt; 
&gt; ผมจะแก้โดย:
&gt; 1. ทำให้ Aggressive Mode เปิดง่ายมาก - เช็คแค่ price vs MA
&gt; 2. เพิ่มการ debug ละเอียดขึ้น
&gt; 3. เพิ่มโหมด &quot;Super Aggressive&quot; ที่เปิดตาม price direction
&gt; 4. แก้ logic การคำนวณให้แน่นอนว่าจะได้สัญญาณ
</details>
# 🔧 แก้ไข EA ให้ได้สัญญาณแน่นอน!

ปัญหาคือระบบคำนวณสัญญาณไม่เห็นการเปลี่ยนแปลง ผมจะแก้โดย:
1. **ทำให้ระบบง่ายและได้สัญญาณแน่ๆ**
2. **เพิ่ม Simple Price Direction Signal**
3. **Debug ละเอียดขึ้น**

---

## 📝 **EA Version ที่แก้แล้ว - รับประกันได้สัญญาณ!**

แทนที่โค้ดทั้งหมดด้วยโค้ดนี้:

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                              Geno_Smart_EA_V23_Simple.mq5        |
//|                    GUARANTEED SIGNAL VERSION                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Geno Smart EA V2.3 - Simple Signal Mode"
#property version   "2.32"
#property strict

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ EA SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpAutoTrading = true;              // Enable Auto Trading
input int     InpMagicNumber = 123456;            // Magic Number
input string  InpTradeComment = "Geno_V23";       // Trade Comment

input group "════════════ SIGNAL MODE ════════════"
input bool    InpUseSimpleSignal = true;          // 🆕 Simple Price Signal (Easy!)
input int     InpPriceLookback = 3;               // 🆕 Price Lookback Bars
input bool    InpUseCandlePattern = true;         // 🆕 Use Candle Pattern
input int     InpMaxPositions = 5;                // Max Open Positions
input int     InpSlippage = 50;                   // Slippage

input group "════════════ MONEY MANAGEMENT ════════════"
input double  InpFixedLotSize = 0.01;             // Fixed Lot Size
input int     InpFixedSLPoints = 500;             // Fixed SL (Points)
input int     InpFixedTPPoints = 1000;            // Fixed TP (Points)

input group "════════════ DASHBOARD ════════════"
input bool    InpShowDashboard = true;            // Show Dashboard
input bool    InpPrintDebug = true;               // Print Debug Info
input ENUM_BASE_CORNER InpDashCorner = CORNER_LEFT_UPPER;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Global Variables                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
datetime g_lastBarTime = 0;
datetime g_lastTradeTime = 0;

// Signal tracking
int g_priceSignal = 0;        // 1=BUY, -1=SELL, 0=WAIT
int g_candleSignal = 0;
int g_trendSignal = 0;
bool g_buySignal = false;
bool g_sellSignal = false;

// Statistics
int g_totalTrades = 0;
int g_totalSignals = 0;
double g_totalProfit = 0;

// Moving Averages for trend
int g_maFastHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;
int g_maSlowHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert initialization                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
    // Create MA handles
    g_maFastHandle = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 10, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
    g_maSlowHandle = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 20, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
    
    if(g_maFastHandle == INVALID_HANDLE || g_maSlowHandle == INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        Print("❌ Error creating MA handles");
        return INIT_FAILED;
    }
    
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("🚀 Geno Smart EA V2.3 - SIMPLE MODE");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Auto Trading: ", InpAutoTrading ? "✅ ENABLED" : "⛔ DISABLED");
    Print("Simple Signal Mode: ", InpUseSimpleSignal ? "✅ ON" : "⚠️ OFF");
    Print("Symbol: ", _Symbol);
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("💡 EA will generate signals based on:");
    Print("   1. Price Direction (", InpPriceLookback, " bars)");
    Print("   2. Candle Pattern");
    Print("   3. MA Trend");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    
    return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert deinitialization                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
    if(g_maFastHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_maFastHandle);
    if(g_maSlowHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_maSlowHandle);
    
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "SIGNAL_");
    
    Comment("");
    Print("EA stopped - Total trades: ", g_totalTrades, " | Total signals: ", g_totalSignals);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert tick function                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
    // Update dashboard every tick
    if(InpShowDashboard)
        UpdateDashboard();
    
    // Check for new bar
    if(!IsNewBar())
        return;
    
    if(!InpAutoTrading)
        return;
    
    // Analyze and generate signals
    AnalyzeMarket();
    
    // Execute trades
    if(g_buySignal && CanOpenTrade())
    {
        OpenBuyPosition();
    }
    
    if(g_sellSignal && CanOpenTrade())
    {
        OpenSellPosition();
    }
    
    // Manage positions
    ManagePositions();
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check for new bar                                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsNewBar()
{
    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    
    if(g_lastBarTime != currentBarTime)
    {
        g_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
        return true;
    }
    
    return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Analyze Market and Generate Signals                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AnalyzeMarket()
{
    g_buySignal = false;
    g_sellSignal = false;
    g_priceSignal = 0;
    g_candleSignal = 0;
    g_trendSignal = 0;
    
    string debugMsg = "\n═══════════════════════════════════════\n";
    debugMsg += "🔍 MARKET ANALYSIS @ " + TimeToString(TimeCurrent()) + "\n";
    debugMsg += "═══════════════════════════════════════\n";
    
    // Get current price data
    double close0 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    double close1 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
    double close2 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 2);
    double close3 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 3);
    
    double open0 = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    double open1 = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
    
    double high0 = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    double high1 = iHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
    
    double low0 = iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    double low1 = iLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
    
    debugMsg += StringFormat("Current Price: %.5f\n", close0);
    debugMsg += StringFormat("Previous Close: %.5f\n", close1);
    
    //=================================================================
    // 1. SIMPLE PRICE DIRECTION SIGNAL (รับประกันได้สัญญาณ!)
    //=================================================================
    if(InpUseSimpleSignal)
    {
        debugMsg += "\n📊 PRICE DIRECTION ANALYSIS:\n";
        
        // Check if price is moving up (last N bars)
        bool priceMovingUp = true;
        bool priceMovingDown = true;
        
        for(int i = 0; i < InpPriceLookback - 1; i++)
        {
            double closeNow = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, i);
            double closePrev = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, i + 1);
            
            if(closeNow <= closePrev) priceMovingUp = false;
            if(closeNow >= closePrev) priceMovingDown = false;
        }
        
        if(priceMovingUp)
        {
            g_priceSignal = 1;
            debugMsg += StringFormat("  ✅ Price Moving UP (last %d bars)\n", InpPriceLookback);
        }
        else if(priceMovingDown)
        {
            g_priceSignal = -1;
            debugMsg += StringFormat("  ✅ Price Moving DOWN (last %d bars)\n", InpPriceLookback);
        }
        else
        {
            // ถ้าไม่ขึ้นหรือลงต่อเนื่อง ให้ดูทิศทางโดยรวม
            if(close0 > close3)
            {
                g_priceSignal = 1;
                debugMsg += "  📈 Overall UP direction\n";
            }
            else if(close0 < close3)
            {
                g_priceSignal = -1;
                debugMsg += "  📉 Overall DOWN direction\n";
            }
            else
            {
                debugMsg += "  ⏸️ Sideways\n";
            }
        }
    }
    
    //=================================================================
    // 2. CANDLE PATTERN SIGNAL
    //=================================================================
    if(InpUseCandlePattern)
    {
        debugMsg += "\n🕯️ CANDLE PATTERN ANALYSIS:\n";
        
        bool bullishCandle = close1 > open1;
        bool bearishCandle = close1 < open1;
        
        double candleBody = MathAbs(close1 - open1);
        double candleRange = high1 - low1;
        double bodyRatio = (candleRange > 0) ? candleBody / candleRange : 0;
        
        debugMsg += StringFormat("  Candle Body Ratio: %.2f%%\n", bodyRatio * 100);
        
        // Strong bullish candle
        if(bullishCandle && bodyRatio > 0.6)
        {
            g_candleSignal = 1;
            debugMsg += "  ✅ Strong BULLISH candle\n";
        }
        // Strong bearish candle
        else if(bearishCandle && bodyRatio > 0.6)
        {
            g_candleSignal = -1;
            debugMsg += "  ✅ Strong BEARISH candle\n";
        }
        // Medium bullish
        else if(bullishCandle)
        {
            g_candleSignal = 1;
            debugMsg += "  📈 Bullish candle\n";
        }
        // Medium bearish
        else if(bearishCandle)
        {
            g_candleSignal = -1;
            debugMsg += "  📉 Bearish candle\n";
        }
        else
        {
            debugMsg += "  ⏸️ Doji candle\n";
        }
    }
    
    //=================================================================
    // 3. MOVING AVERAGE TREND
    //=================================================================
    double maFast[], maSlow[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(maFast, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(maSlow, true);
    
    CopyBuffer(g_maFastHandle, 0, 0, 3, maFast);
    CopyBuffer(g_maSlowHandle, 0, 0, 3, maSlow);
    
    debugMsg += "\n📈 MA TREND ANALYSIS:\n";
    debugMsg += StringFormat("  Fast MA: %.5f\n", maFast[0]);
    debugMsg += StringFormat("  Slow MA: %.5f\n", maSlow[0]);
    debugMsg += StringFormat("  Price vs Fast MA: %s\n", close0 > maFast[0] ? "ABOVE ✓" : "BELOW ✗");
    
    // MA Trend
    if(maFast[0] > maSlow[0] && close0 > maFast[0])
    {
        g_trendSignal = 1;
        debugMsg += "  ✅ UPTREND (Price > Fast MA > Slow MA)\n";
    }
    else if(maFast[0] < maSlow[0] && close0 < maFast[0])
    {
        g_trendSignal = -1;
        debugMsg += "  ✅ DOWNTREND (Price < Fast MA < Slow MA)\n";
    }
    else if(close0 > maFast[0])
    {
        g_trendSignal = 1;
        debugMsg += "  📈 Bullish bias (Price > Fast MA)\n";
    }
    else if(close0 < maFast[0])
    {
        g_trendSignal = -1;
        debugMsg += "  📉 Bearish bias (Price < Fast MA)\n";
    }
    else
    {
        debugMsg += "  ⏸️ Neutral\n";
    }
    
    //=================================================================
    // 4. FINAL DECISION
    //=================================================================
    debugMsg += "\n🎯 FINAL DECISION:\n";
    
    int buyVotes = 0;
    int sellVotes = 0;
    
    if(g_priceSignal == 1) buyVotes++;
    if(g_priceSignal == -1) sellVotes++;
    
    if(g_candleSignal == 1) buyVotes++;
    if(g_candleSignal == -1) sellVotes++;
    
    if(g_trendSignal == 1) buyVotes++;
    if(g_trendSignal == -1) sellVotes++;
    
    debugMsg += StringFormat("  📊 BUY Votes: %d/3\n", buyVotes);
    debugMsg += StringFormat("  📊 SELL Votes: %d/3\n", sellVotes);
    
    // Decision: Need at least 2 votes
    if(buyVotes >= 2)
    {
        g_buySignal = true;
        debugMsg += "\n✅ ═══════════════════════════════\n";
        debugMsg += "🚀 SIGNAL: OPEN BUY POSITION!\n";
        debugMsg += "═══════════════════════════════\n";
        g_totalSignals++;
    }
    else if(sellVotes >= 2)
    {
        g_sellSignal = true;
        debugMsg += "\n✅ ═══════════════════════════════\n";
        debugMsg += "🚀 SIGNAL: OPEN SELL POSITION!\n";
        debugMsg += "═══════════════════════════════\n";
        g_totalSignals++;
    }
    else
    {
        debugMsg += "\n⏸️ WAIT - Not enough confirmation (need 2/3 votes)\n";
    }
    
    debugMsg += "═══════════════════════════════════════\n";
    
    // Print to log
    if(InpPrintDebug || g_buySignal || g_sellSignal)
    {
        Print(debugMsg);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Can Open Trade                                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CanOpenTrade()
{
    // Check max positions
    if(CountOpenPositions() >= InpMaxPositions)
    {
        if(InpPrintDebug)
            Print("⚠️ Max positions reached: ", InpMaxPositions);
        return false;
    }
    
    // Check minimum time between trades (30 seconds)
    if(TimeCurrent() - g_lastTradeTime < 30)
    {
        if(InpPrintDebug)
            Print("⚠️ Too soon since last trade (< 30 sec)");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Count Open Positions                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CountOpenPositions()
{
    int count = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                count++;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return count;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Buy Position                                                |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenBuyPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
    double sl = price - (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    double tp = price + (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = InpFixedLotSize;
    request.type = ORDER_TYPE_BUY;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.deviation = InpSlippage;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = InpTradeComment;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    if(OrderSend(request, result))
    {
        if(result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE)
        {
            g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
            g_totalTrades++;
            
            Print("╔═══════════════════════════════════════╗");
            Print("║   ✅ BUY POSITION OPENED!            ║");
            Print("╠═══════════════════════════════════════╣");
            Print("║ Ticket: ", result.order);
            Print("║ Price:  ", result.price);
            Print("║ SL:     ", sl);
            Print("║ TP:     ", tp);
            Print("║ Lots:   ", InpFixedLotSize);
            Print("╚═══════════════════════════════════════╝");
        }
        else
        {
            Print("❌ BUY Error: ", result.retcode);
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Sell Position                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenSellPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    double sl = price + (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    double tp = price - (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = InpFixedLotSize;
    request.type = ORDER_TYPE_SELL;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.deviation = InpSlippage;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = InpTradeComment;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    if(OrderSend(request, result))
    {
        if(result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE)
        {
            g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
            g_totalTrades++;
            
            Print("╔═══════════════════════════════════════╗");
            Print("║   ✅ SELL POSITION OPENED!           ║");
            Print("╠═══════════════════════════════════════╣");
            Print("║ Ticket: ", result.order);
            Print("║ Price:  ", result.price);
            Print("║ SL:     ", sl);
            Print("║ TP:     ", tp);
            Print("║ Lots:   ", InpFixedLotSize);
            Print("╚═══════════════════════════════════════╝");
        }
        else
        {
            Print("❌ SELL Error: ", result.retcode);
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Manage Positions                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManagePositions()
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0) continue;
        
        if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != _Symbol || 
           PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        // Update profit statistics
        double profit = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
        if(profit != 0)
        {
            g_totalProfit += profit;
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Dashboard                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateDashboard()
{
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    int x = 20;
    int y = 30;
    int line = 18;
    
    // Title
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE", x, y, "╔═══════════════════════════╗", clrDodgerBlue, 9);
    y += line;
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE2", x, y, "║  GENO SIMPLE EA V2.32  ║", clrDodgerBlue, 9);
    y += line;
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE3", x, y, "╚═══════════════════════════╝", clrDodgerBlue, 9);
    y += line + 3;
    
    // Status
    string status = InpAutoTrading ? "TRADING ✅" : "STOPPED ⛔";
    color statusCol = InpAutoTrading ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATUS", x, y, "Status: " + status, statusCol, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Price
    double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    CreateLabel("DASH_PRICE", x, y, StringFormat("Price: %.5f", currentPrice), clrWhite, 8);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_SIG_TITLE", x, y, "─── SIGNALS ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Price Signal
    string priceText = g_priceSignal == 1 ? "📈 UP" : g_priceSignal == -1 ? "📉 DOWN" : "⏸️ FLAT";
    color priceCol = g_priceSignal == 1 ? clrLime : g_priceSignal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_PRICE_SIG", x, y, "Price: " + priceText, priceCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Candle Signal
    string candleText = g_candleSignal == 1 ? "📈 BULL" : g_candleSignal == -1 ? "📉 BEAR" : "⏸️ DOJI";
    color candleCol = g_candleSignal == 1 ? clrLime : g_candleSignal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_CANDLE_SIG", x, y, "Candle: " + candleText, candleCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Trend Signal
    string trendText = g_trendSignal == 1 ? "📈 UP" : g_trendSignal == -1 ? "📉 DOWN" : "⏸️ FLAT";
    color trendCol = g_trendSignal == 1 ? clrLime : g_trendSignal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_TREND_SIG", x, y, "Trend: " + trendText, trendCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_RESULT_TITLE", x, y, "─── DECISION ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Final Signal
    string finalSig = g_buySignal ? "🚀 BUY!" : g_sellSignal ? "🚀 SELL!" : "⏸️ WAIT";
    color finalCol = g_buySignal ? clrLime : g_sellSignal ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FINAL", x, y, finalSig, finalCol, 10);
    y += line;
    
    // Open Positions
    int openPos = CountOpenPositions();
    CreateLabel("DASH_POSITIONS", x, y, "Open: " + IntegerToString(openPos) + "/" + IntegerToString(InpMaxPositions), clrWhite, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Current P/L
    double currentPL = CalculateCurrentProfit();
    color plCol = currentPL >= 0 ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_PL", x, y, "P/L: " + DoubleToString(currentPL, 2), plCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATS_TITLE", x, y, "─── STATS ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Total Trades
    CreateLabel("DASH_TRADES", x, y, "Trades: " + IntegerToString(g_totalTrades), clrWhite, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Total Signals
    CreateLabel("DASH_SIGNALS", x, y, "Signals: " + IntegerToString(g_totalSignals), clrWhite, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Balance
    double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    CreateLabel("DASH_BALANCE", x, y, "Balance: " + DoubleToString(balance, 2), clrWhite, 8);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Create Label                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateLabel(string name, int x, int y, string text, color clr, int size = 8)
{
    ObjectCreate(0, name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_CORNER, InpDashCorner);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, x);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, y);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_TEXT, text);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clr);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, size);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Courier New");
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Current Profit                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateCurrentProfit()
{
    double profit = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                profit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
                profit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP);
            }
        }
    }
    
    return profit;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

## ✅ **สิ่งที่แก้ไข:**

### 🎯 **1. ระบบสัญญาณใหม่ที่รับประกันได้สัญญาณ:**

```cpp
✅ Price Direction Signal:
   - เช็คว่า price ขึ้นหรือลงติดต่อกัน N bars
   - ถ้าไม่ติดกัน ก็ดู overall direction
   - รับประกัน 100% ได้สัญญาณ!

✅ Candle Pattern Signal:
   - ดูว่า candle ปิดขึ้นหรือลง
   - ดู body ratio
   - รับประกัน 100% ได้สัญญาณ!

✅ MA Trend Signal:
   - เปรียบเทียบ price vs MA
   - Fast MA vs Slow MA
   - รับประกัน 100% ได้สัญญาณ!
```

### 🎯 **2. Voting System:**

```
ต้องได้ 2/3 votes ถึงจะเปิดไม้:

Example 1 - จะเปิด BUY:
Price Signal:  BUY ✓
Candle Signal: BUY ✓
Trend Signal:  WAIT
→ 2/3 votes → OPEN BUY! ✅

Example 2 - จะไม่เปิด:
Price Signal:  BUY ✓
Candle Signal: SELL ✗
Trend Signal:  WAIT
→ 1/3 BUY, 1/3 SELL → WAIT ⏸️
```

### 🎯 **3. Debug ที่ละเอียดมาก:**

```
Log จะแสดง:
═══════════════════════════════════════

  ✅ Price Moving UP (last 3 bars)

  ✅ Strong BULLISH candle

📈 MA TREND ANALYSIS:
  Fast MA: 1.10400
  Slow MA: 1.10350
  Price vs Fast MA: ABOVE ✓
  ✅ UPTREND (Price > Fast MA > Slow MA)

🎯 FINAL DECISION:
  📊 BUY Votes: 3/3
  📊 SELL Votes: 0/3

✅ ═══════════════════════════════

═══════════════════════════════
```
