# 🔧 แก้ไข EA - ลบ Recovery Mode & Emergency Stop

ผมจะลบส่วนที่เกี่ยวข้องทั้งหมดออกให้ครับ

---

## 📝 **EA Version ใหม่ (ไม่มี Recovery & Emergency)**

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                          Geno_Martingale_EA_Clean.mq5            |
//|                    Martingale + Grid (No Recovery)               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Geno Martingale EA - Clean Version"
#property version   "3.10"
#property strict

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - EA Settings                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ EA SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpAutoTrading = true;              // Enable Auto Trading
input int     InpMagicNumber = 123456;            // Magic Number
input string  InpTradeComment = "Geno_Mart";      // Trade Comment

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Money Management Settings                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ 💰 MONEY MANAGEMENT ════════════"
input double  InpInitialLot = 0.01;               // Initial Lot Size
input bool    InpUseMartingale = true;            // Use Martingale
input double  InpMartingaleMultiplier = 2.0;      // Martingale Multiplier (1.5-3.0)
input int     InpMaxMartingaleLevel = 5;          // Max Martingale Level (1-10)

input bool    InpUseGrid = true;                  // Use Grid Trading
input int     InpGridDistance = 200;              // Grid Distance (Points)
input double  InpGridLotIncrement = 0.01;         // Grid Lot Increment
input int     InpMaxGridLevels = 10;              // Max Grid Levels

input bool    InpUseVirtualSL = true;             // Use Virtual SL (No Hard SL)
input int     InpVirtualSLPoints = 1000;          // Virtual SL Distance (Points)

input double  InpMaxLotSize = 10.0;               // Maximum Lot Size (Safety)
input double  InpMinLotSize = 0.01;               // Minimum Lot Size

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Take Profit Settings                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ 🎯 TAKE PROFIT ════════════"
input bool    InpUseBasketTP = true;              // Use Basket TP (Total Profit)
input double  InpBasketTPMoney = 10.0;            // Basket TP in Money
input double  InpBasketTPPercent = 0.0;           // Basket TP in % (0=disabled)

input bool    InpUseSingleTP = false;             // Use Single TP per trade
input int     InpSingleTPPoints = 500;            // Single TP (Points)

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Signal Settings                                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ 📊 SIGNAL SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpUseSimpleSignal = true;          // Simple Price Signal
input int     InpPriceLookback = 3;               // Price Lookback Bars
input bool    InpUseCandlePattern = true;         // Use Candle Pattern

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Dashboard & Debug                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ 📺 DASHBOARD ════════════"
input bool    InpShowDashboard = true;            // Show Dashboard
input bool    InpPrintDebug = true;               // Print Debug Info
input ENUM_BASE_CORNER InpDashCorner = CORNER_LEFT_UPPER;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Global Variables                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
datetime g_lastBarTime = 0;
datetime g_lastTradeTime = 0;

// Signal Variables
bool g_buySignal = false;
bool g_sellSignal = false;
int g_priceSignal = 0;
int g_candleSignal = 0;

// Money Management Variables
double g_currentLotSize = 0.01;
int g_currentMartingaleLevel = 0;
int g_currentGridLevel = 0;
double g_lastBuyPrice = 0;
double g_lastSellPrice = 0;

// Statistics
int g_totalTrades = 0;
int g_winTrades = 0;
int g_loseTrades = 0;
double g_totalProfit = 0;
double g_totalLoss = 0;
double g_startingBalance = 0;

// Position Tracking
struct PositionInfo {
    ulong ticket;
    datetime openTime;
    double openPrice;
    double lotSize;
    int level;
    bool isGrid;
    ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type;
};

PositionInfo g_positions[];

// MA Handles
int g_maFastHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;
int g_maSlowHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert initialization                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
    // Validate settings
    if(InpMartingaleMultiplier < 1.0 || InpMartingaleMultiplier > 5.0)
    {
        Print("❌ Invalid Martingale Multiplier! Must be 1.0-5.0");
        return INIT_PARAMETERS_INCORRECT;
    }
    
    if(InpMaxMartingaleLevel < 1 || InpMaxMartingaleLevel > 20)
    {
        Print("❌ Invalid Max Martingale Level! Must be 1-20");
        return INIT_PARAMETERS_INCORRECT;
    }
    
    // Create MA handles
    g_maFastHandle = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 10, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
    g_maSlowHandle = iMA(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 20, 0, MODE_EMA, PRICE_CLOSE);
    
    if(g_maFastHandle == INVALID_HANDLE || g_maSlowHandle == INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        Print("❌ Error creating MA handles");
        return INIT_FAILED;
    }
    
    // Initialize variables
    g_currentLotSize = InpInitialLot;
    g_startingBalance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    
    ArrayResize(g_positions, 0);
    
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("🎰 Geno Martingale EA V3.1 Started");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("⚠️  HIGH RISK SYSTEM - USE WITH CAUTION!");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Auto Trading: ", InpAutoTrading ? "✅ ENABLED" : "⛔ DISABLED");
    Print("Starting Balance: ", g_startingBalance);
    Print("Initial Lot: ", InpInitialLot);
    Print("Martingale: ", InpUseMartingale ? "ON" : "OFF");
    Print("Martingale Multiplier: ", InpMartingaleMultiplier);
    Print("Grid Trading: ", InpUseGrid ? "ON" : "OFF");
    Print("Grid Distance: ", InpGridDistance, " points");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    
    return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert deinitialization                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
    if(g_maFastHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_maFastHandle);
    if(g_maSlowHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_maSlowHandle);
    
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    Comment("");
    
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("EA Stopped - Final Statistics:");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Total Trades: ", g_totalTrades);
    Print("Win: ", g_winTrades, " | Loss: ", g_loseTrades);
    Print("Win Rate: ", g_totalTrades > 0 ? DoubleToString((double)g_winTrades / g_totalTrades * 100, 1) : "0", "%");
    Print("Total Profit: ", g_totalProfit);
    Print("Total Loss: ", g_totalLoss);
    Print("Net Profit: ", g_totalProfit - g_totalLoss);
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert tick function                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
    // Update position tracking
    UpdatePositionTracking();
    
    // Update dashboard
    if(InpShowDashboard)
        UpdateDashboard();
    
    // Check for new bar
    if(!IsNewBar())
        return;
    
    if(!InpAutoTrading)
        return;
    
    // Check basket TP
    if(InpUseBasketTP)
        CheckBasketTP();
    
    // Check virtual SL for all positions
    if(InpUseVirtualSL)
        CheckVirtualSL();
    
    // Analyze market
    AnalyzeMarket();
    
    // Manage grid positions
    if(InpUseGrid)
        ManageGridPositions();
    
    // Open new positions based on signals
    if(g_buySignal || g_sellSignal)
    {
        if(CanOpenNewPosition())
        {
            if(g_buySignal)
                OpenPosition(POSITION_TYPE_BUY);
            
            if(g_sellSignal)
                OpenPosition(POSITION_TYPE_SELL);
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if new bar                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsNewBar()
{
    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    
    if(g_lastBarTime != currentBarTime)
    {
        g_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
        return true;
    }
    
    return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Analyze Market                                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void AnalyzeMarket()
{
    g_buySignal = false;
    g_sellSignal = false;
    g_priceSignal = 0;
    g_candleSignal = 0;
    
    // Get price data
    double close0 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    double close1 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
    double close3 = iClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 3);
    
    double open1 = iOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 1);
    
    // Price Direction Signal
    if(InpUseSimpleSignal)
    {
        if(close0 > close3)
            g_priceSignal = 1;
        else if(close0 < close3)
            g_priceSignal = -1;
    }
    
    // Candle Pattern Signal
    if(InpUseCandlePattern)
    {
        if(close1 > open1)
            g_candleSignal = 1;
        else if(close1 < open1)
            g_candleSignal = -1;
    }
    
    // Get MA values
    double maFast[], maSlow[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(maFast, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(maSlow, true);
    
    CopyBuffer(g_maFastHandle, 0, 0, 2, maFast);
    CopyBuffer(g_maSlowHandle, 0, 0, 2, maSlow);
    
    // Final decision
    int buyVotes = 0;
    int sellVotes = 0;
    
    if(g_priceSignal == 1) buyVotes++;
    if(g_priceSignal == -1) sellVotes++;
    
    if(g_candleSignal == 1) buyVotes++;
    if(g_candleSignal == -1) sellVotes++;
    
    if(close0 > maFast[0] && maFast[0] > maSlow[0]) buyVotes++;
    if(close0 < maFast[0] && maFast[0] < maSlow[0]) sellVotes++;
    
    if(buyVotes >= 2) g_buySignal = true;
    if(sellVotes >= 2) g_sellSignal = true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Can Open New Position                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CanOpenNewPosition()
{
    // Check if enough time passed since last trade
    if(TimeCurrent() - g_lastTradeTime < 30)
        return false;
    
    return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Lot Size                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateLotSize(ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type)
{
    double lotSize = InpInitialLot;
    
    // Count losing streak
    int losingStreak = CountLosingStreak(type);
    
    // Apply Martingale
    if(InpUseMartingale && losingStreak > 0)
    {
        int level = MathMin(losingStreak, InpMaxMartingaleLevel);
        lotSize = InpInitialLot * MathPow(InpMartingaleMultiplier, level);
        
        if(InpPrintDebug)
        {
            Print("🎲 Martingale Level: ", level);
            Print("   Losing Streak: ", losingStreak);
            Print("   New Lot Size: ", lotSize);
        }
    }
    
    // Safety limits
    lotSize = MathMax(lotSize, InpMinLotSize);
    lotSize = MathMin(lotSize, InpMaxLotSize);
    
    // Normalize
    lotSize = NormalizeLotSize(lotSize);
    
    return lotSize;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Count Losing Streak                                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CountLosingStreak(ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type)
{
    int streak = 0;
    
    HistorySelect(0, TimeCurrent());
    
    for(int i = HistoryDealsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = HistoryDealGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0) continue;
        
        if(HistoryDealGetString(ticket, DEAL_SYMBOL) != _Symbol)
            continue;
        
        if(HistoryDealGetInteger(ticket, DEAL_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        if(HistoryDealGetInteger(ticket, DEAL_ENTRY) != DEAL_ENTRY_OUT)
            continue;
        
        ENUM_DEAL_TYPE dealType = (ENUM_DEAL_TYPE)HistoryDealGetInteger(ticket, DEAL_TYPE);
        
        bool sameDirection = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY && dealType == DEAL_TYPE_BUY) ||
                            (type == POSITION_TYPE_SELL && dealType == DEAL_TYPE_SELL);
        
        if(!sameDirection)
            continue;
        
        double profit = HistoryDealGetDouble(ticket, DEAL_PROFIT);
        
        if(profit < 0)
        {
            streak++;
        }
        else
        {
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return streak;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Position                                                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenPosition(ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type)
{
    double price = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? 
                   SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK) : 
                   SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    
    double lotSize = CalculateLotSize(type);
    
    double sl = 0;
    double tp = 0;
    
    if(!InpUseVirtualSL)
    {
        if(type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
            sl = price - (InpVirtualSLPoints * _Point);
        else
            sl = price + (InpVirtualSLPoints * _Point);
        
        sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    }
    
    if(InpUseSingleTP)
    {
        if(type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
            tp = price + (InpSingleTPPoints * _Point);
        else
            tp = price - (InpSingleTPPoints * _Point);
        
        tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    }
    
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = lotSize;
    request.type = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? ORDER_TYPE_BUY : ORDER_TYPE_SELL;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.deviation = 50;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = InpTradeComment;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    bool orderResult = OrderSend(request, result);
    
    if(orderResult)
    {
        if(result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE || result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_PLACED)
        {
            g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
            g_totalTrades++;
            
            if(type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
                g_lastBuyPrice = price;
            else
                g_lastSellPrice = price;
            
            Print("╔═══════════════════════════════════════╗");
            Print("║   ✅ ", type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY ? "BUY" : "SELL", " POSITION OPENED");
            Print("╠═══════════════════════════════════════╣");
            Print("║ Ticket: ", result.order);
            Print("║ Price:  ", result.price);
            Print("║ Lots:   ", lotSize);
            Print("║ Level:  ", CountLosingStreak(type));
            Print("╚═══════════════════════════════════════╝");
        }
        else
        {
            Print("❌ Order failed: ", result.retcode);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        Print("❌ OrderSend failed: ", GetLastError());
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Manage Grid Positions                                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManageGridPositions()
{
    double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    
    int buyPositions = 0;
    int sellPositions = 0;
    
    for(int i = 0; i < ArraySize(g_positions); i++)
    {
        if(g_positions[i].type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
            buyPositions++;
        else
            sellPositions++;
    }
    
    // BUY Grid
    if(g_lastBuyPrice > 0 && buyPositions < InpMaxGridLevels)
    {
        double distanceFromLast = g_lastBuyPrice - currentPrice;
        
        if(distanceFromLast >= InpGridDistance * _Point)
        {
            double gridLot = InpInitialLot + (buyPositions * InpGridLotIncrement);
            gridLot = NormalizeLotSize(gridLot);
            
            if(InpPrintDebug)
            {
                Print("📊 Opening BUY Grid Level ", buyPositions + 1);
                Print("   Distance: ", distanceFromLast / _Point, " points");
                Print("   Lot Size: ", gridLot);
            }
            
            OpenGridPosition(POSITION_TYPE_BUY, gridLot);
        }
    }
    
    // SELL Grid
    if(g_lastSellPrice > 0 && sellPositions < InpMaxGridLevels)
    {
        double distanceFromLast = currentPrice - g_lastSellPrice;
        
        if(distanceFromLast >= InpGridDistance * _Point)
        {
            double gridLot = InpInitialLot + (sellPositions * InpGridLotIncrement);
            gridLot = NormalizeLotSize(gridLot);
            
            if(InpPrintDebug)
            {
                Print("📊 Opening SELL Grid Level ", sellPositions + 1);
                Print("   Distance: ", distanceFromLast / _Point, " points");
                Print("   Lot Size: ", gridLot);
            }
            
            OpenGridPosition(POSITION_TYPE_SELL, gridLot);
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Grid Position                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenGridPosition(ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type, double lotSize)
{
    double price = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? 
                   SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK) : 
                   SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = lotSize;
    request.type = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? ORDER_TYPE_BUY : ORDER_TYPE_SELL;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = 0;
    request.tp = 0;
    request.deviation = 50;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = "GRID";
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    bool orderResult = OrderSend(request, result);
    
    if(orderResult && (result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE || result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_PLACED))
    {
        if(type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
            g_lastBuyPrice = price;
        else
            g_lastSellPrice = price;
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check Basket TP                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckBasketTP()
{
    double totalProfit = CalculateTotalProfit();
    
    double targetProfit = InpBasketTPMoney;
    
    if(InpBasketTPPercent > 0)
    {
        double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
        targetProfit = balance * (InpBasketTPPercent / 100.0);
    }
    
    if(totalProfit >= targetProfit)
    {
        Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
        Print("✅ BASKET TAKE PROFIT HIT!");
        Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
        Print("Total Profit: ", totalProfit);
        Print("Target Profit: ", targetProfit);
        Print("Closing all positions...");
        Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
        
        CloseAllPositions();
        
        // Reset tracking
        g_lastBuyPrice = 0;
        g_lastSellPrice = 0;
        g_currentMartingaleLevel = 0;
        g_currentGridLevel = 0;
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check Virtual SL                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckVirtualSL()
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0) continue;
        
        if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != _Symbol ||
           PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        double openPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
        double currentPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_CURRENT);
        ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type = (ENUM_POSITION_TYPE)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
        
        double lossPoints = 0;
        
        if(type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
            lossPoints = (openPrice - currentPrice) / _Point;
        else
            lossPoints = (currentPrice - openPrice) / _Point;
        
        if(lossPoints >= InpVirtualSLPoints)
        {
            if(InpPrintDebug)
            {
                Print("🚫 Virtual SL triggered for ticket ", ticket);
                Print("   Loss: ", lossPoints, " points");
            }
            
            ClosePosition(ticket);
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Total Profit                                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateTotalProfit()
{
    double totalProfit = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                totalProfit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
                totalProfit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP);
            }
        }
    }
    
    return totalProfit;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Close Position                                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ClosePosition(ulong ticket)
{
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket))
        return;
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.position = ticket;
    request.symbol = PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL);
    request.volume = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
    request.type = (PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE) == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? 
                   ORDER_TYPE_SELL : ORDER_TYPE_BUY;
    request.price = (request.type == ORDER_TYPE_BUY) ? 
                    SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK) : 
                    SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    request.deviation = 50;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    OrderSend(request, result);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Close All Positions                                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CloseAllPositions()
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                ClosePosition(ticket);
            }
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Position Tracking                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdatePositionTracking()
{
    ArrayResize(g_positions, 0);
    
    for(int i = 0; i < PositionsTotal(); i++)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0) continue;
        
        if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != _Symbol ||
           PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        int size = ArraySize(g_positions);
        ArrayResize(g_positions, size + 1);
        
        g_positions[size].ticket = ticket;
        g_positions[size].openTime = (datetime)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TIME);
        g_positions[size].openPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
        g_positions[size].lotSize = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
        g_positions[size].type = (ENUM_POSITION_TYPE)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
        g_positions[size].isGrid = (PositionGetString(POSITION_COMMENT) == "GRID");
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Normalize Lot Size                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double NormalizeLotSize(double lot)
{
    double minLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
    double maxLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
    double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
    
    lot = MathMax(lot, minLot);
    lot = MathMin(lot, maxLot);
    
    if(lotStep > 0)
        lot = MathRound(lot / lotStep) * lotStep;
    
    return NormalizeDouble(lot, 2);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Dashboard                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateDashboard()
{
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    int x = 20;
    int y = 30;
    int line = 18;
    
    // Title
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE", x, y, "╔══════════════════════════╗", clrDodgerBlue, 9);
    y += line;
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE2", x, y, "║  MARTINGALE EA V3.1   ║", clrDodgerBlue, 9);
    y += line;
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE3", x, y, "╚══════════════════════════╝", clrDodgerBlue, 9);
    y += line + 3;
    
    // Status
    string status = InpAutoTrading ? "ACTIVE ✅" : "STOPPED ⛔";
    color statusCol = InpAutoTrading ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATUS", x, y, "Status: " + status, statusCol, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Price
    double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    CreateLabel("DASH_PRICE", x, y, StringFormat("Price: %.5f", currentPrice), clrWhite, 8);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_SIG_TITLE", x, y, "─── SIGNALS ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Price Signal
    string priceText = g_priceSignal == 1 ? "📈 UP" : g_priceSignal == -1 ? "📉 DOWN" : "⏸️ FLAT";
    color priceCol = g_priceSignal == 1 ? clrLime : g_priceSignal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_PRICE_SIG", x, y, "Price: " + priceText, priceCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Candle Signal
    string candleText = g_candleSignal == 1 ? "📈 BULL" : g_candleSignal == -1 ? "📉 BEAR" : "⏸️ DOJI";
    color candleCol = g_candleSignal == 1 ? clrLime : g_candleSignal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_CANDLE_SIG", x, y, "Candle: " + candleText, candleCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Final Signal
    string finalSig = g_buySignal ? "🚀 BUY!" : g_sellSignal ? "🚀 SELL!" : "⏸️ WAIT";
    color finalCol = g_buySignal ? clrLime : g_sellSignal ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FINAL", x, y, finalSig, finalCol, 10);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_INFO_TITLE", x, y, "─── POSITIONS ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Open Positions
    int openPos = ArraySize(g_positions);
    int buyPos = 0, sellPos = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < openPos; i++)
    {
        if(g_positions[i].type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) buyPos++;
        else sellPos++;
    }
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_POSITIONS", x, y, StringFormat("Open: %d (B:%d S:%d)", openPos, buyPos, sellPos), clrWhite, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Current Profit
    double totalProfit = CalculateTotalProfit();
    color profitCol = totalProfit >= 0 ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_PROFIT", x, y, StringFormat("P/L: %.2f", totalProfit), profitCol, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Basket TP Target
    if(InpUseBasketTP)
    {
        double target = InpBasketTPMoney;
        if(InpBasketTPPercent > 0)
            target = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE) * (InpBasketTPPercent / 100.0);
        
        double progress = (target > 0) ? (totalProfit / target * 100.0) : 0;
        CreateLabel("DASH_TARGET", x, y, StringFormat("Target: %.2f (%.1f%%)", target, progress), clrYellow, 8);
        y += line;
    }
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_INFO_TITLE2", x, y, "─── INFO ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Martingale Info
    if(InpUseMartingale)
    {
        int buyStreak = CountLosingStreak(POSITION_TYPE_BUY);
        int sellStreak = CountLosingStreak(POSITION_TYPE_SELL);
        
        CreateLabel("DASH_MART", x, y, StringFormat("Mart: B:%d S:%d", buyStreak, sellStreak), clrWhite, 7);
        y += line - 2;
    }
    
    // Grid Info
    if(InpUseGrid)
    {
        CreateLabel("DASH_GRID", x, y, StringFormat("Grid: %d pts", InpGridDistance), clrWhite, 7);
        y += line - 2;
    }
    
    // Stats
    CreateLabel("DASH_TRADES", x, y, StringFormat("Trades: %d", g_totalTrades), clrWhite, 7);
    y += line - 2;
    
    double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    double equity = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_EQUITY);
    CreateLabel("DASH_BAL", x, y, StringFormat("Bal: %.2f", balance), clrWhite, 7);
    y += line - 2;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_EQ", x, y, StringFormat("Eq: %.2f", equity), equity >= balance ? clrLime : clrRed, 7);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Create Label                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateLabel(string name, int x, int y, string text, color clr, int size = 8)
{
    ObjectCreate(0, name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_CORNER, InpDashCorner);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, x);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, y);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_TEXT, text);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clr);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, size);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Courier New");
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

## 🎯 **ระบบที่เหลืออยู่:**

### ✅ **1. Martingale**
```
- เพิ่ม lot เมื่อขาดทุน
- ทบตาม Multiplier
- จำกัดด้วย Max Level
```

### ✅ **2. Grid Trading**
```
- เปิดออเดอร์ทุก X points
- เพิ่ม lot ทีละ 0.01
- จำกัดด้วย Max Grid Levels
```

### ✅ **3. Virtual Stop Loss**
```
- ไม่มี SL บน chart
- ปิดเมื่อขาดทุนถึง X points
- ป้องกัน SL Hunt
```

### ✅ **4. Basket Take Profit**
```
- รวมกำไรทุกออเดอร์
- ปิดพร้อมกันเมื่อถึงเป้า
- ตั้งเป้าเป็น $ หรือ %
```

### ✅ **5. Signal System**
```
- Price Direction
- Candle Pattern
- MA Trend
- Voting System (2/3)
```

## 💡 **ข้อดีของการลบ Recovery & Emergency:**

### **✅ ข้อดี:**
1. **ง่ายขึ้น** - ไม่ซับซ้อน
2. **ควบคุมได้ง่าย** - ไม่มีโหมดพิเศษ
3. **ใช้ margin น้อยลง** - ไม่มีการทบเพิ่ม
4. **เข้าใจง่าย** - logic ตรงไปตรงมา

### **⚠️ ข้อควรระวัง:**
1. **ไม่มีระบบกู้คืน** - ถ้าขาดทุนมาก ต้องพึ่ง Martingale + Grid
2. **ไม่มี Emergency Stop** - ต้อง monitor เอง
3. **ความเสี่ยงยังสูง** - Martingale + Grid ยังมีความเสี่ยง

---

## 🎯 **แนะนำเพิ่มเติม:**

เนื่องจากไม่มี Emergency Stop แล้ว แนะนำให้:

1. **Set Max DD ใน Broker** (ถ้าทำได้)
2. **Monitor ทุกวัน**
3. **ตั้ง Equity Stop Loss** ใน MT5
4. **ใช้ lot เล็กๆ**
5. **ทดสอบใน Demo นานๆ**

# 🔄 EA กลับไปใช้หลักการเดิม (จาก Indicator V2.3)

เข้าใจแล้วครับ! ผมจะทำ EA ที่ใช้หลักการจาก **Indicator V2.3 ตัวเดิม** ที่คุณส่งมา

---

## 📝 **EA Version Original Concept**

```mql5
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//|                          Geno_Smart_EA_Original.mq5              |
//|                   Based on Smart Signal V2.3 Indicator           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
#property copyright "Geno Smart EA - Original Concept"
#property version   "2.40"
#property strict

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Input Parameters - EA Settings                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ EA SETTINGS ════════════"
input bool    InpAutoTrading = true;              // Enable Auto Trading
input int     InpMagicNumber = 223456;            // Magic Number
input string  InpTradeComment = "Geno_Smart";     // Trade Comment
input int     InpMaxPositions = 1;                // Max Open Positions
input bool    InpCloseOppositeSignal = true;      // Close Opposite Signal

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Smart Trail Settings (จาก Indicator เดิม)                       |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ SMART TRAIL ════════════"
input bool    InpUseSmartTrail = true;            // Use Smart Trail Signal
input string  InpTrailType = "modified";          // Trail Type
input int     InpATRPeriod = 200;                 // ATR Period
input double  InpATRFactor = 4.2;                 // ATR Factor
input int     InpSmoothing = 4;                   // Smoothing

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Follow Line Settings                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ FOLLOW LINE ════════════"
input bool    InpUseFollowLine1 = true;           // Use Follow Line 1
input int     InpBBPeriod1 = 21;                  // BB Period 1
input double  InpBBDeviation1 = 1.0;              // BB Deviations 1
input bool    InpUseATRFilter1 = true;            // Use ATR Filter 1
input int     InpATRPeriod1 = 5;                  // ATR Period 1

input bool    InpUseFollowLine2 = true;           // Use Follow Line 2
input bool    InpUseCurrentTF = true;             // Use Current TF
input int     InpMAPeriod = 21;                   // MA Period
input ENUM_MA_METHOD InpMAMethod = MODE_EMA;      // MA Method
input double  InpBBDeviations2 = 1.0;             // BB Deviations 2
input int     InpATRPeriod2 = 5;                  // ATR Period 2

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Signal Confirmation                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ SIGNAL SETTINGS ════════════"
input int     InpMinSignalConfirmation = 2;       // Min Signals (1-3)

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Money Management (จาก Indicator เดิม)                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ MONEY MANAGEMENT ════════════"
input bool    InpUseAutoLotSize = true;           // Use Auto Lot Calculation
input double  InpFixedLotSize = 0.01;             // Fixed Lot Size
input string  InpAccountCur = "USD";              // Account Currency
input int     InpUnitsLot = 100;                  // Units/Lot (XAU=100)
input double  InpRiskPercent = 1.0;               // Risk % per Trade
input double  InpMaxLotSize = 10.0;               // Maximum Lot Size
input double  InpMinLotSize = 0.01;               // Minimum Lot Size

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Stop Loss & Take Profit (จาก Indicator เดิม)                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ STOP LOSS & TAKE PROFIT ════════════"
input bool    InpUseDynamicSL = true;             // Dynamic SL (Swing Points)
input int     InpSLBuffer = 100;                  // SL Buffer (Points)
input int     InpMinSLPoints = 100;               // Min SL (Points)
input int     InpMaxSLPoints = 3000;              // Max SL (Points)
input int     InpFixedSLPoints = 500;             // Fixed SL (Points)

input bool    InpUseDynamicTP = true;             // Dynamic TP (Risk:Reward)
input double  InpRiskRewardRatio = 2.0;           // Risk:Reward Ratio
input int     InpFixedTPPoints = 1000;            // Fixed TP (Points)

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Trailing Stop                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ TRAILING STOP ════════════"
input bool    InpUseTrailingStop = true;          // Enable Trailing Stop
input bool    InpTrailFollowLine = true;          // Follow Smart Trail Line
input int     InpTrailingStart = 200;             // Trailing Start (Points)
input int     InpTrailingStep = 100;              // Trailing Step (Points)

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Dashboard                                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
input group "════════════ DASHBOARD ════════════"
input bool    InpShowDashboard = true;            // Show Dashboard
input ENUM_BASE_CORNER InpDashCorner = CORNER_LEFT_UPPER;
input color   InpDashTitleCol = clrDodgerBlue;    // Title Color
input color   InpDashTextCol = clrWhite;          // Text Color

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Global Variables                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
// Indicator Handles
int g_atrHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;
int g_atrHandle1 = INVALID_HANDLE;
int g_atrHandle2 = INVALID_HANDLE;
int g_maHandle = INVALID_HANDLE;

// Smart Trail Variables
double g_trailBuffer[];
double g_trendUpBuffer[];
double g_trendDownBuffer[];

// Follow Line Variables
double g_trendLine1Buffer[];
double g_trendLineBuffer[];

// Swing Points
double g_lastSwingHigh = 0;
double g_lastSwingLow = 0;

// Signal Variables
bool g_buySignal = false;
bool g_sellSignal = false;
int g_smartTrailSignal = 0;
int g_followLine1Signal = 0;
int g_followLine2Signal = 0;

// Trade Variables
datetime g_lastBarTime = 0;
datetime g_lastTradeTime = 0;
double g_entryPrice = 0;
double g_stopLoss = 0;
double g_takeProfit = 0;
double g_currentLotSize = 0;

// Statistics
int g_totalTrades = 0;
int g_winTrades = 0;
int g_loseTrades = 0;
double g_totalProfit = 0;
double g_startingBalance = 0;

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert initialization function                                    |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int OnInit()
{
    // Create indicator handles
    g_atrHandle = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod);
    g_atrHandle1 = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod1);
    g_atrHandle2 = iATR(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, InpATRPeriod2);
    
    ENUM_TIMEFRAMES tf = InpUseCurrentTF ? PERIOD_CURRENT : PERIOD_H1;
    g_maHandle = iMA(_Symbol, tf, InpMAPeriod, 0, InpMAMethod, PRICE_CLOSE);
    
    if(g_atrHandle == INVALID_HANDLE || g_atrHandle1 == INVALID_HANDLE || 
       g_atrHandle2 == INVALID_HANDLE || g_maHandle == INVALID_HANDLE)
    {
        Print("❌ Error creating indicator handles");
        return INIT_FAILED;
    }
    
    // Initialize buffers
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trailBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendUpBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendDownBuffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendLine1Buffer, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(g_trendLineBuffer, true);
    
    ArrayResize(g_trailBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendUpBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendDownBuffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendLine1Buffer, 500);
    ArrayResize(g_trendLineBuffer, 500);
    
    ArrayInitialize(g_trailBuffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendUpBuffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendDownBuffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendLine1Buffer, 0);
    ArrayInitialize(g_trendLineBuffer, 0);
    
    g_startingBalance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("🚀 Geno Smart EA V2.4 - Original Concept");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Auto Trading: ", InpAutoTrading ? "✅ ENABLED" : "⛔ DISABLED");
    Print("Starting Balance: ", g_startingBalance);
    Print("Smart Trail: ", InpUseSmartTrail ? "ON" : "OFF");
    Print("Follow Line 1: ", InpUseFollowLine1 ? "ON" : "OFF");
    Print("Follow Line 2: ", InpUseFollowLine2 ? "ON" : "OFF");
    Print("Min Signal Confirmation: ", InpMinSignalConfirmation);
    Print("Auto Lot: ", InpUseAutoLotSize ? "ON" : "OFF");
    Print("Risk %: ", InpRiskPercent);
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    
    return INIT_SUCCEEDED;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert deinitialization function                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnDeinit(const int reason)
{
    if(g_atrHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_atrHandle);
    if(g_atrHandle1 != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_atrHandle1);
    if(g_atrHandle2 != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_atrHandle2);
    if(g_maHandle != INVALID_HANDLE) IndicatorRelease(g_maHandle);
    
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    Comment("");
    
    double finalBalance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    double netProfit = finalBalance - g_startingBalance;
    double winRate = g_totalTrades > 0 ? (double)g_winTrades / g_totalTrades * 100 : 0;
    
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("EA Stopped - Final Report:");
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
    Print("Total Trades: ", g_totalTrades);
    Print("Win: ", g_winTrades, " | Loss: ", g_loseTrades);
    Print("Win Rate: ", DoubleToString(winRate, 1), "%");
    Print("Net Profit: ", DoubleToString(netProfit, 2));
    Print("═══════════════════════════════════════");
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Expert tick function                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OnTick()
{
    // Update dashboard every tick
    if(InpShowDashboard)
        UpdateDashboard();
    
    // Check for new bar
    if(!IsNewBar())
        return;
    
    if(!InpAutoTrading)
        return;
    
    // Calculate indicators (จาก Indicator เดิม)
    CalculateIndicators();
    
    // Check for trading signals
    CheckForSignals();
    
    // Manage open positions
    ManagePositions();
    
    // Execute trades
    if(g_buySignal && CanOpenTrade())
    {
        if(InpCloseOppositeSignal)
            ClosePositions(POSITION_TYPE_SELL);
        
        OpenBuyPosition();
    }
    
    if(g_sellSignal && CanOpenTrade())
    {
        if(InpCloseOppositeSignal)
            ClosePositions(POSITION_TYPE_BUY);
        
        OpenSellPosition();
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check if new bar                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool IsNewBar()
{
    datetime currentBarTime = iTime(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0);
    
    if(g_lastBarTime != currentBarTime)
    {
        g_lastBarTime = currentBarTime;
        return true;
    }
    
    return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Indicators (จาก Indicator V2.3)                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateIndicators()
{
    // Get price data
    double high[], low[], close[], open[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(high, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(low, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(close, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(open, true);
    
    int bars = 100;
    CopyHigh(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, high);
    CopyLow(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, low);
    CopyClose(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, close);
    CopyOpen(_Symbol, PERIOD_CURRENT, 0, bars, open);
    
    // Get ATR values
    double atr[], atr1[], atr2[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr1, true);
    ArraySetAsSeries(atr2, true);
    
    CopyBuffer(g_atrHandle, 0, 0, bars, atr);
    CopyBuffer(g_atrHandle1, 0, 0, bars, atr1);
    CopyBuffer(g_atrHandle2, 0, 0, bars, atr2);
    
    // Get MA values
    double ma[];
    ArraySetAsSeries(ma, true);
    CopyBuffer(g_maHandle, 0, 0, bars, ma);
    
    // Calculate Smart Trail
    if(InpUseSmartTrail)
        CalculateSmartTrail(high, low, close, atr);
    
    // Calculate Follow Line 1
    if(InpUseFollowLine1)
        CalculateFollowLine1(high, low, close, atr1);
    
    // Calculate Follow Line 2
    if(InpUseFollowLine2)
        CalculateFollowLine2(high, low, close, ma, atr2);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Smart Trail (จาก Indicator)                            |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateSmartTrail(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                         const double &close[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    // Calculate True Range
    double trueRange = CalculateTrueRange(shift, high, low, close);
    
    // Calculate Wild MA
    static double wild_prev = 0;
    double wild = (wild_prev == 0) ? trueRange : wild_prev + (trueRange - wild_prev) / InpATRPeriod;
    wild_prev = wild;
    
    double loss = InpATRFactor * wild;
    
    // Calculate Up and Down levels
    double Up68 = close[shift] - loss;
    double Dn68 = close[shift] + loss;
    
    // Calculate Trend
    if(shift == 0 && g_trendUpBuffer[1] != 0)
    {
        if(close[1] > g_trendUpBuffer[1])
            g_trendUpBuffer[0] = MathMax(Up68, g_trendUpBuffer[1]);
        else
            g_trendUpBuffer[0] = Up68;
        
        if(close[1] < g_trendDownBuffer[1])
            g_trendDownBuffer[0] = MathMin(Dn68, g_trendDownBuffer[1]);
        else
            g_trendDownBuffer[0] = Dn68;
    }
    else
    {
        g_trendUpBuffer[0] = Up68;
        g_trendDownBuffer[0] = Dn68;
    }
    
    // Determine trend
    int trend = 0;
    if(g_trailBuffer[1] != 0)
    {
        if(close[0] > g_trendDownBuffer[1])
            trend = 1;
        else if(close[0] < g_trendUpBuffer[1])
            trend = -1;
        else
            trend = (g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendUpBuffer[1]) ? 1 : -1;
    }
    else
    {
        trend = (close[0] > (Up68 + Dn68) / 2) ? 1 : -1;
    }
    
    g_trailBuffer[0] = (trend == 1) ? g_trendUpBuffer[0] : g_trendDownBuffer[0];
    
    // Update swing points (สำหรับ SL)
    if(trend == 1)
        g_lastSwingLow = g_trailBuffer[0];
    else
        g_lastSwingHigh = g_trailBuffer[0];
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate True Range                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateTrueRange(int shift, const double &high[], 
                          const double &low[], const double &close[])
{
    if(shift >= ArraySize(high) - 1)
        return high[shift] - low[shift];
    
    double tr1 = high[shift] - low[shift];
    double tr2 = MathAbs(high[shift] - close[shift+1]);
    double tr3 = MathAbs(low[shift] - close[shift+1]);
    
    return MathMax(tr1, MathMax(tr2, tr3));
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 1                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine1(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                          const double &close[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    // Calculate BB
    double sma = 0;
    double sum = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < InpBBPeriod1; i++)
    {
        sma += close[shift + i];
    }
    sma /= InpBBPeriod1;
    
    for(int i = 0; i < InpBBPeriod1; i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[shift + i] - sma, 2);
    }
    double stdev = MathSqrt(sum / InpBBPeriod1);
    
    double BBUpper = sma + stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    double BBLower = sma - stdev * InpBBDeviation1;
    
    // Calculate TrendLine
    static double TrendLine1_prev = 0;
    
    double TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    int BBSignal = 0;
    
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper)
        BBSignal = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower)
        BBSignal = -1;
    
    if(BBSignal == 1)
    {
        TrendLine1 = InpUseATRFilter1 ? (low[shift] - atr[shift]) : low[shift];
        if(TrendLine1_prev != 0 && TrendLine1 < TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal == -1)
    {
        TrendLine1 = InpUseATRFilter1 ? (high[shift] + atr[shift]) : high[shift];
        if(TrendLine1_prev != 0 && TrendLine1 > TrendLine1_prev)
            TrendLine1 = TrendLine1_prev;
    }
    
    if(TrendLine1 == 0)
        TrendLine1 = close[shift];
    
    g_trendLine1Buffer[0] = TrendLine1;
    TrendLine1_prev = TrendLine1;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Follow Line 2                                          |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CalculateFollowLine2(const double &high[], const double &low[], 
                          const double &close[], const double &ma[], const double &atr[])
{
    int shift = 0;
    
    // Calculate BB based on MA
    double sum = 0;
    for(int i = 0; i < InpMAPeriod; i++)
    {
        sum += MathPow(close[shift + i] - ma[shift], 2);
    }
    double stdev = MathSqrt(sum / InpMAPeriod);
    
    double BBUpper = ma[shift] + stdev * InpBBDeviations2;
    double BBLower = ma[shift] - stdev * InpBBDeviations2;
    
    // Calculate TrendLine
    static double TrendLine_prev = 0;
    static int iTrend_prev = 0;
    
    double TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    int BBSignal = 0;
    
    if(close[shift] > BBUpper)
        BBSignal = 1;
    else if(close[shift] < BBLower)
        BBSignal = -1;
    
    double v = atr[shift];
    
    if(BBSignal == 1)
    {
        TrendLine = low[shift] - v;
        if(TrendLine_prev != 0 && TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    else if(BBSignal == -1)
    {
        TrendLine = high[shift] + v;
        if(TrendLine_prev != 0 && TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
            TrendLine = TrendLine_prev;
    }
    
    if(TrendLine == 0)
        TrendLine = close[shift];
    
    int iTrend = iTrend_prev;
    if(TrendLine_prev != 0)
    {
        if(TrendLine > TrendLine_prev)
            iTrend = 1;
        else if(TrendLine < TrendLine_prev)
            iTrend = -1;
    }
    
    g_trendLineBuffer[0] = TrendLine;
    
    // Update swing points
    if(iTrend_prev == -1 && iTrend == 1)
        g_lastSwingLow = TrendLine;
    
    if(iTrend_prev == 1 && iTrend == -1)
        g_lastSwingHigh = TrendLine;
    
    TrendLine_prev = TrendLine;
    iTrend_prev = iTrend;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Check For Trading Signals                                        |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CheckForSignals()
{
    g_buySignal = false;
    g_sellSignal = false;
    
    int buyVotes = 0;
    int sellVotes = 0;
    
    g_smartTrailSignal = 0;
    g_followLine1Signal = 0;
    g_followLine2Signal = 0;
    
    // Check Smart Trail Signal
    if(InpUseSmartTrail && g_trailBuffer[1] != 0 && g_trailBuffer[2] != 0)
    {
        // Trail crossed up
        if(g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendUpBuffer[1] && g_trailBuffer[2] == g_trendDownBuffer[2])
        {
            g_smartTrailSignal = 1;
            buyVotes++;
        }
        // Trail crossed down
        else if(g_trailBuffer[1] == g_trendDownBuffer[1] && g_trailBuffer[2] == g_trendUpBuffer[2])
        {
            g_smartTrailSignal = -1;
            sellVotes++;
        }
    }
    
    // Check Follow Line 1 Signal
    if(InpUseFollowLine1 && g_trendLine1Buffer[1] != 0 && g_trendLine1Buffer[2] != 0)
    {
        if(g_trendLine1Buffer[1] > g_trendLine1Buffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine1Signal = 1;
            buyVotes++;
        }
        else if(g_trendLine1Buffer[1] < g_trendLine1Buffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine1Signal = -1;
            sellVotes++;
        }
    }
    
    // Check Follow Line 2 Signal
    if(InpUseFollowLine2 && g_trendLineBuffer[1] != 0 && g_trendLineBuffer[2] != 0)
    {
        if(g_trendLineBuffer[1] > g_trendLineBuffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine2Signal = 1;
            buyVotes++;
        }
        else if(g_trendLineBuffer[1] < g_trendLineBuffer[2])
        {
            g_followLine2Signal = -1;
            sellVotes++;
        }
    }
    
    // Final Decision
    if(buyVotes >= InpMinSignalConfirmation)
        g_buySignal = true;
    
    if(sellVotes >= InpMinSignalConfirmation)
        g_sellSignal = true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Can Open Trade                                                   |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool CanOpenTrade()
{
    // Check max positions
    if(CountOpenPositions() >= InpMaxPositions)
        return false;
    
    // Check minimum time between trades
    if(TimeCurrent() - g_lastTradeTime < 60)
        return false;
    
    return true;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Count Open Positions                                             |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
int CountOpenPositions()
{
    int count = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                count++;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return count;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Buy Position (ตาม Indicator เดิม)                           |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenBuyPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
    double sl = 0, tp = 0;
    
    // Calculate SL (Dynamic from Swing Low)
    if(InpUseDynamicSL && g_lastSwingLow > 0)
    {
        sl = g_lastSwingLow - (InpSLBuffer * _Point);
        
        // Validate SL
        double slDistance = price - sl;
        if(slDistance < InpMinSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price - (InpMinSLPoints * _Point);
        if(slDistance > InpMaxSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price - (InpMaxSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    else
    {
        sl = price - (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Calculate TP (Dynamic R:R)
    if(InpUseDynamicTP)
    {
        double slDistance = price - sl;
        tp = price + (slDistance * InpRiskRewardRatio);
    }
    else
    {
        tp = price + (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    // Calculate Lot Size (Auto Lot Calculator จาก Indicator)
    double lotSize = InpFixedLotSize;
    if(InpUseAutoLotSize)
    {
        lotSize = CalculateLotSize(price, sl);
    }
    
    lotSize = NormalizeLotSize(lotSize);
    
    // Open Position
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = lotSize;
    request.type = ORDER_TYPE_BUY;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.deviation = 50;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = InpTradeComment;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    bool orderResult = OrderSend(request, result);
    
    if(orderResult && (result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE || result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_PLACED))
    {
        g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
        g_totalTrades++;
        g_entryPrice = price;
        g_stopLoss = sl;
        g_takeProfit = tp;
        g_currentLotSize = lotSize;
        
        Print("╔═══════════════════════════════════════╗");
        Print("║   ✅ BUY POSITION OPENED              ║");
        Print("╠═══════════════════════════════════════╣");
        Print("║ Ticket: ", result.order);
        Print("║ Price:  ", price);
        Print("║ SL:     ", sl, " (", DoubleToString((price - sl) / _Point, 0), " pts)");
        Print("║ TP:     ", tp, " (", DoubleToString((tp - price) / _Point, 0), " pts)");
        Print("║ Lots:   ", lotSize);
        Print("║ Risk:   ", DoubleToString(InpRiskPercent, 1), "%");
        Print("║ R:R:    1:", DoubleToString(InpRiskRewardRatio, 1));
        Print("╚═══════════════════════════════════════╝");
    }
    else
    {
        Print("❌ BUY Error: ", result.retcode);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Open Sell Position                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void OpenSellPosition()
{
    double price = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
    double sl = 0, tp = 0;
    
    // Calculate SL (Dynamic from Swing High)
    if(InpUseDynamicSL && g_lastSwingHigh > 0)
    {
        sl = g_lastSwingHigh + (InpSLBuffer * _Point);
        
        // Validate SL
        double slDistance = sl - price;
        if(slDistance < InpMinSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price + (InpMinSLPoints * _Point);
        if(slDistance > InpMaxSLPoints * _Point)
            sl = price + (InpMaxSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    else
    {
        sl = price + (InpFixedSLPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    // Calculate TP
    if(InpUseDynamicTP)
    {
        double slDistance = sl - price;
        tp = price - (slDistance * InpRiskRewardRatio);
    }
    else
    {
        tp = price - (InpFixedTPPoints * _Point);
    }
    
    sl = NormalizeDouble(sl, _Digits);
    tp = NormalizeDouble(tp, _Digits);
    
    // Calculate Lot Size
    double lotSize = InpFixedLotSize;
    if(InpUseAutoLotSize)
    {
        lotSize = CalculateLotSize(price, sl);
    }
    
    lotSize = NormalizeLotSize(lotSize);
    
    // Open Position
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    request.volume = lotSize;
    request.type = ORDER_TYPE_SELL;
    request.price = price;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.deviation = 50;
    request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
    request.comment = InpTradeComment;
    request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
    
    bool orderResult = OrderSend(request, result);
    
    if(orderResult && (result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE || result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_PLACED))
    {
        g_lastTradeTime = TimeCurrent();
        g_totalTrades++;
        g_entryPrice = price;
        g_stopLoss = sl;
        g_takeProfit = tp;
        g_currentLotSize = lotSize;
        
        Print("╔═══════════════════════════════════════╗");
        Print("║   ✅ SELL POSITION OPENED             ║");
        Print("╠═══════════════════════════════════════╣");
        Print("║ Ticket: ", result.order);
        Print("║ Price:  ", price);
        Print("║ SL:     ", sl, " (", DoubleToString((sl - price) / _Point, 0), " pts)");
        Print("║ TP:     ", tp, " (", DoubleToString((price - tp) / _Point, 0), " pts)");
        Print("║ Lots:   ", lotSize);
        Print("║ Risk:   ", DoubleToString(InpRiskPercent, 1), "%");
        Print("║ R:R:    1:", DoubleToString(InpRiskRewardRatio, 1));
        Print("╚═══════════════════════════════════════╝");
    }
    else
    {
        Print("❌ SELL Error: ", result.retcode);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Lot Size (จาก Indicator เดิม)                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateLotSize(double entryPrice, double stopLoss)
{
    double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    double riskAmount = balance * (InpRiskPercent / 100.0);
    double slDistance = MathAbs(entryPrice - stopLoss);
    
    if(slDistance == 0)
        return InpMinLotSize;
    
    // Get conversion rate if needed
    double conversionRate = 1.0;
    string accountCur = AccountInfoString(ACCOUNT_CURRENCY);
    
    if(accountCur != InpAccountCur)
    {
        string pair = InpAccountCur + accountCur;
        conversionRate = SymbolInfoDouble(pair, SYMBOL_BID);
        if(conversionRate == 0)
            conversionRate = 1.0 / SymbolInfoDouble(accountCur + InpAccountCur, SYMBOL_BID);
    }
    
    // Calculate lot
    double tickValue = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_VALUE);
    double tickSize = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_TRADE_TICK_SIZE);
    
    if(tickSize == 0 || tickValue == 0)
        return InpMinLotSize;
    
    double lotSize = (riskAmount * tickSize) / (slDistance * tickValue * InpUnitsLot);
    
    if(conversionRate != 1.0)
        lotSize *= conversionRate;
    
    return lotSize;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Normalize Lot Size                                               |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double NormalizeLotSize(double lot)
{
    double minLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MIN);
    double maxLot = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_MAX);
    double lotStep = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_VOLUME_STEP);
    
    lot = MathMax(lot, InpMinLotSize);
    lot = MathMin(lot, InpMaxLotSize);
    lot = MathMax(lot, minLot);
    lot = MathMin(lot, maxLot);
    
    if(lotStep > 0)
        lot = MathRound(lot / lotStep) * lotStep;
    
    return NormalizeDouble(lot, 2);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Manage Positions (Trailing Stop)                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ManagePositions()
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0) continue;
        
        if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != _Symbol || 
           PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        if(InpUseTrailingStop)
            ApplyTrailingStop(ticket);
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Apply Trailing Stop                                              |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ApplyTrailingStop(ulong ticket)
{
    if(!PositionSelectByTicket(ticket))
        return;
    
    double currentSL = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SL);
    double currentTP = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_TP);
    double openPrice = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PRICE_OPEN);
    ENUM_POSITION_TYPE posType = (ENUM_POSITION_TYPE)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
    
    double newSL = currentSL;
    
    if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_BUY)
    {
        double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID);
        double profit = currentPrice - openPrice;
        
        if(profit >= InpTrailingStart * _Point)
        {
            if(InpTrailFollowLine && g_trailBuffer[0] > 0)
            {
                newSL = g_trailBuffer[0];
            }
            else
            {
                newSL = currentPrice - (InpTrailingStep * _Point);
            }
            
            newSL = NormalizeDouble(newSL, _Digits);
            
            if(newSL > currentSL && newSL < currentPrice)
            {
                ModifyPosition(ticket, newSL, currentTP);
            }
        }
    }
    else if(posType == POSITION_TYPE_SELL)
    {
        double currentPrice = SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
        double profit = openPrice - currentPrice;
        
        if(profit >= InpTrailingStart * _Point)
        {
            if(InpTrailFollowLine && g_trailBuffer[0] > 0)
            {
                newSL = g_trailBuffer[0];
            }
            else
            {
                newSL = currentPrice + (InpTrailingStep * _Point);
            }
            
            newSL = NormalizeDouble(newSL, _Digits);
            
            if((newSL < currentSL || currentSL == 0) && newSL > currentPrice)
            {
                ModifyPosition(ticket, newSL, currentTP);
            }
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Modify Position                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
bool ModifyPosition(ulong ticket, double sl, double tp)
{
    MqlTradeRequest request = {};
    MqlTradeResult result = {};
    
    request.action = TRADE_ACTION_SLTP;
    request.position = ticket;
    request.sl = sl;
    request.tp = tp;
    request.symbol = _Symbol;
    
    if(OrderSend(request, result))
    {
        if(result.retcode == TRADE_RETCODE_DONE)
        {
            Print("✓ Trailing Stop Updated: SL=", sl);
            return true;
        }
    }
    
    return false;
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Close Positions                                                  |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void ClosePositions(ENUM_POSITION_TYPE type)
{
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket <= 0) continue;
        
        if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) != _Symbol || 
           PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) != InpMagicNumber)
            continue;
        
        ENUM_POSITION_TYPE posType = (ENUM_POSITION_TYPE)PositionGetInteger(POSITION_TYPE);
        
        if(posType == type)
        {
            MqlTradeRequest request = {};
            MqlTradeResult result = {};
            
            request.action = TRADE_ACTION_DEAL;
            request.position = ticket;
            request.symbol = _Symbol;
            request.volume = PositionGetDouble(POSITION_VOLUME);
            request.type = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? ORDER_TYPE_SELL : ORDER_TYPE_BUY;
            request.price = (type == POSITION_TYPE_BUY) ? 
                           SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_BID) : 
                           SymbolInfoDouble(_Symbol, SYMBOL_ASK);
            request.deviation = 50;
            request.magic = InpMagicNumber;
            request.type_filling = ORDER_FILLING_FOK;
            
            if(OrderSend(request, result))
            {
                Print("✓ Position closed by opposite signal");
            }
        }
    }
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Update Dashboard                                                 |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void UpdateDashboard()
{
    ObjectsDeleteAll(0, "DASH_");
    
    int x = 20;
    int y = 30;
    int line = 18;
    
    // Title
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE", x, y, "╔═══════════════════════════╗", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += line;
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE2", x, y, "║ GENO SMART EA V2.4     ║", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += line;
    CreateLabel("DASH_TITLE3", x, y, "╚═══════════════════════════╝", InpDashTitleCol, 9);
    y += line + 3;
    
    // Status
    string status = InpAutoTrading ? "ACTIVE ✅" : "STOPPED ⛔";
    color statusCol = InpAutoTrading ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATUS", x, y, "Status: " + status, statusCol, 9);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_SIG_TITLE", x, y, "─── SIGNALS ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Smart Trail
    string stText = g_smartTrailSignal == 1 ? "📈 BUY" : g_smartTrailSignal == -1 ? "📉 SELL" : "⏸️ WAIT";
    color stCol = g_smartTrailSignal == 1 ? clrLime : g_smartTrailSignal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_ST", x, y, "Smart Trail: " + stText, stCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Follow Line 1
    string fl1Text = g_followLine1Signal == 1 ? "📈 BUY" : g_followLine1Signal == -1 ? "📉 SELL" : "⏸️ WAIT";
    color fl1Col = g_followLine1Signal == 1 ? clrLime : g_followLine1Signal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FL1", x, y, "Follow Line 1: " + fl1Text, fl1Col, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Follow Line 2
    string fl2Text = g_followLine2Signal == 1 ? "📈 BUY" : g_followLine2Signal == -1 ? "📉 SELL" : "⏸️ WAIT";
    color fl2Col = g_followLine2Signal == 1 ? clrLime : g_followLine2Signal == -1 ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FL2", x, y, "Follow Line 2: " + fl2Text, fl2Col, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Final Signal
    string finalSig = g_buySignal ? "🚀 BUY!" : g_sellSignal ? "🚀 SELL!" : "⏸️ WAIT";
    color finalCol = g_buySignal ? clrLime : g_sellSignal ? clrRed : clrGray;
    CreateLabel("DASH_FINAL", x, y, finalSig, finalCol, 10);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_INFO_TITLE", x, y, "─── POSITIONS ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Open Positions
    int openPos = CountOpenPositions();
    CreateLabel("DASH_POS", x, y, "Open: " + IntegerToString(openPos), InpDashTextCol, 8);
    y += line;
    
    // Current P/L
    double currentPL = CalculateCurrentProfit();
    color plCol = currentPL >= 0 ? clrLime : clrRed;
    CreateLabel("DASH_PL", x, y, StringFormat("P/L: %.2f", currentPL), plCol, 9);
    y += line;
    
    y += 3;
    CreateLabel("DASH_STATS_TITLE", x, y, "─── STATS ───", clrYellow, 9);
    y += line;
    
    // Stats
    CreateLabel("DASH_TRADES", x, y, "Trades: " + IntegerToString(g_totalTrades), InpDashTextCol, 7);
    y += line - 2;
    
    double winRate = g_totalTrades > 0 ? (double)g_winTrades / g_totalTrades * 100 : 0;
    CreateLabel("DASH_WR", x, y, StringFormat("Win Rate: %.1f%%", winRate), InpDashTextCol, 7);
    y += line - 2;
    
    double balance = AccountInfoDouble(ACCOUNT_BALANCE);
    CreateLabel("DASH_BAL", x, y, StringFormat("Balance: %.2f", balance), InpDashTextCol, 7);
    y += line - 2;
    
    // Swing Points
    CreateLabel("DASH_SWING_HIGH", x, y, StringFormat("Swing H: %.5f", g_lastSwingHigh), clrOrange, 7);
    y += line - 2;
    
    CreateLabel("DASH_SWING_LOW", x, y, StringFormat("Swing L: %.5f", g_lastSwingLow), clrAqua, 7);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Create Label                                                     |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
void CreateLabel(string name, int x, int y, string text, color clr, int size = 8)
{
    ObjectCreate(0, name, OBJ_LABEL, 0, 0, 0);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_CORNER, InpDashCorner);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_XDISTANCE, x);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_YDISTANCE, y);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_TEXT, text);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_COLOR, clr);
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_FONTSIZE, size);
    ObjectSetString(0, name, OBJPROP_FONT, "Arial Bold");
    ObjectSetInteger(0, name, OBJPROP_SELECTABLE, false);
}

//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
//| Calculate Current Profit                                         |
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
double CalculateCurrentProfit()
{
    double profit = 0;
    
    for(int i = PositionsTotal() - 1; i >= 0; i--)
    {
        ulong ticket = PositionGetTicket(i);
        if(ticket > 0)
        {
            if(PositionGetString(POSITION_SYMBOL) == _Symbol && 
               PositionGetInteger(POSITION_MAGIC) == InpMagicNumber)
            {
                profit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_PROFIT);
                profit += PositionGetDouble(POSITION_SWAP);
            }
        }
    }
    
    return profit;
}
//+------------------------------------------------------------------+
```

## 🎯 **หลักการทำงาน:**

### **📊 Signal Generation:**
```
1. Smart Trail คำนวณ Supertrend
2. Follow Line 1 ใช้ BB + ATR
3. Follow Line 2 ใช้ MA + BB
4. นับ votes จาก 3 ระบบ
5. ถ้าได้ ≥ 2 votes → เปิดไม้
```

### **💰 Position Management:**
```
1. คำนวณ Lot ตาม Risk %
2. SL จาก Swing Point
3. TP ตาม R:R (1:2)
4. Trailing Stop ติดตาม Smart Trail
```

### **🎮 Trade Flow:**
```
New Bar
  ↓
Calculate Indicators
  ↓
Check Signals
  ↓
If BUY Signal (2/3 votes)
  ↓
Calculate Lot Size
  ↓
Set SL (Swing Low - Buffer)
  ↓
Set TP (SL Distance × R:R)
  ↓
Open BUY Position
  ↓
Apply Trailing Stop
```
